Result: 作者论证了新解释中"简洁性"之名承担了原本由定理承担的工作——它把一个实质性的、未经论证的假设伪装成熟悉方法论原则（Occam剃刀）的应用，从而在解释上形成了缺口。

Conclusion: 新提出的简洁性解释无法像经典理论那样在理论上为泛化提供保证，其说服力依赖于"简洁性"这一名称的修辞力量而非实质性的数学支撑。这一解释性缺口值得统计学、机器学习和哲学界共同关注。

Abstract: Contemporary deep learning methods generalize well even when they fit their training data perfectly, a phenomenon known as benign interpolation. This phenomenon cannot be accounted for by classical statistical learning theory and has prompted a range of attempted new explanations in the statistics and machine learning literature. A common feature of these new proposals is an appeal to a simplicity preference among interpolating models, often presented as a form of Occam's razor. We clarify this debate for a philosophical audience and argue that this new appeal to simplicity creates an explanatory gap. The classical theory offers theorems which connect the simplicity of model classes to good generalization, thus underwriting methodological simplicity norms. The new accounts instead appeal to properties of individual models, which they interpret as a kind of simplicity. Lacking a provable connection to generalization, it is the name "simplicity" that does the work a theorem used to do, making a substantive and unargued assumption look like the application of a familiar methodological principle.

</details>


### [69] [TimeRLM: Recursive Language Models Enable Precise Anomaly Localization in Long-Context Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03391)
*Nicolas Zumarraga,Lorenzo Steno,Ning Wang,Max Rosenblattl,Thomas Kaar,Maxwell A. Xu,Kevin O'Sullivan,Markus Kreft,Elgar Fleisch,Paul Schmiedmayer,Patrick Langer,Robert Jakob*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeRLM，一种针对长上下文时间序列异常定位的递归语言模型（RLM），通过代码和视觉能力顺序操作信号，将上下文外部化以规避大语言模型在长上下文中的检索性能衰减。同时引入AnomalyXL合成基准（含5类任务和MCQ/Localize两种变体），并在四项Localize任务上以0.682 IoU和0.745 classify-with-evidence显著超越所有单遍基线。经强化学习后训练后性能进一步提升，且收敛所需交互轮数减少约三分之二，在未见过的真实ECG、睡眠和软件可观测性数据上仍保持或提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文时间序列中异常证据常潜藏于高频数据的长跨度内，而TSLM在长上下文下检索性能显著下降，类似于文本、视觉和音频领域中的失败模式。文本领域的RLM通过将上下文外置并用代码查询来恢复丢失的性能，这一思路尚未被应用于时间序列领域。

Method: 提出TimeRLM递归语言模型框架，顺序利用代码和视觉能力操控时间序列信号，将上下文保持在LLM外部。构建AnomalyXL合成基准，包含5类任务（AnomalyXL-MCQ和AnomalyXL-Localize两变体），注入程序化异常以要求精确检索。使用强化学习对TimeRLM进行后训练。

Result: TimeRLM在AnomalyXL-Localize的4/5任务上超越所有TSLM和单遍基线：定位IoU达到0.682，classify-with-evidence达到0.745，而基线最高仅0.329和0.072。RL后训练进一步提升性能，所需agent交互轮数约为未训练基线的三分之一。在未见真实ECG、睡眠和软件可观测性数据上保持或提升性能，超越仅接受合成数据训练的TSLM。

Conclusion: 递归交互（recursive interaction）是长时程时间序列检索的有效范式，即使仅在合成数据上训练，也具有对未见真实场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Precise anomaly localization over long-context time series is a crucial task in monitoring applications across clinical care, industrial operations, financial services, and logistics, where brief evidence may hide inside long spans of high-frequency data. Time-Series Language Models (TSLMs) are able to ingest time series data and verbalize findings on anomalies in natural language; however, recent benchmarks report a decrease in retrieval performance at long contexts, mirroring failure modes in text, vision, and audio. In the text domain, Recursive Language Models (RLMs) can recover much of this lost performance by keeping context external to the large language model (LLM), allowing the model to query it through code. We present TimeRLM, an RLM formulation for time-series that sequentially manipulates the signal using code and vision capabilities. We further introduce AnomalyXL, a synthetic long-context anomaly localization benchmark with programmatically injected anomalies that require precise retrieval. We implement five different task categories and two variants: AnomalyXL-MCQ and AnomalyXL-Localize. TimeRLM outperforms every evaluated TSLM and single-pass baseline on four of the five AnomalyXL-Localize tasks, reaching 0.682 IoU on localization and 0.745 on classify-with-evidence, versus at most 0.329 and 0.072 across all baselines. We post-train TimeRLM using reinforcement learning. The resulting model further improves performance and requires approximately one-third as many agent interaction turns as its untrained base model to produce a final answer. On unseen real-world ECG, sleep and software observability recordings, the post-trained TimeRLM retains or improves performance, surpassing TSLMs despite being trained exclusively on synthetic data. Our findings suggest recursive interaction with time-series is an effective approach for long-horizon retrieval.

</details>


### [70] [Shorter Reasoning, Earlier Answers? An Evaluation of Reasoning Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03401)
*Francesca Carlon,Vincent Ginis,Andres Algaba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了在匹配推理预算（token上限）条件下，不同缩短推理策略（提示词和训练设置的推理强度）对问答准确率的影响，发现紧预算下低推理强度或简洁指令更优，但允许高推理强度完成可恢复更高最终准确率，并建议评测应区分按时完成、截断回答、未完成差异和概率质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常先长推理再作答，导致成本和延迟增加。现有缩短推理的方法（提示词或训练设置）可能只是让模型更早停止，而非真正提高效率。需要在匹配推理预算的前提下公平比较不同策略的真实效果。

Method: 在198个GPQA Diamond和500个MMLU-Pro问题上，对同一问题进行配对运行，匹配推理预算（token限制）。测试两种缩短推理策略：（1）对Qwen3-14B使用数字/简洁提示词宣布token上限；（2）gpt-oss-20b和-120b的训练设置的effort级别（低/中/高）。分别比较完成时准确率、截断运行获得的答案、未完成运行的差异以及正确答案的概率分配。

Result: （1）Qwen的提示词将推理轨迹缩短12-17%，但在匹配token限制下准确率变化小而混合；简洁/早答指令在512 tokens时使MMLU-Pro准确率提高3.8个百分点（其中2.7个百分点来自两边都未完成时），2048 tokens时收益不确定。（2）对于gpt-oss，低/中effort完成的候选logit答案比匹配预算的高effort答案准确率高14.5-26.3个百分点；512 token优势主要来自低effort更早完成，未完成运行间差异较小且混合。（3）错误的早答常把概率集中在所选选项上，因此更早停止并不统一改善概率质量。

Conclusion: 紧推理预算下低effort或简洁指令更有利，而允许高effort完成推理可恢复更高最终准确率。评测应分别报告：截止前正确完成率、截断运行获得的答案、未完成运行间的差异，以及正确答案的概率质量，以更全面地理解缩短推理策略的权衡。

Abstract: Large language models often reason at length before answering, increasing cost and latency. Prompts and trained settings can shorten this reasoning, but a shorter trace may only show that the model stopped sooner. Here, we evaluate paired runs of the same question at matched reasoning horizons across 198 GPQA Diamond and 500 MMLU-Pro questions. We test a numeric/concision prompt that announces a token limit for Qwen3-14B and the trained effort settings of gpt-oss-20b and -120b. The Qwen prompt shortens reasoning traces by 12-17%, while accuracy changes at matched token limits are small and mixed. A concise/early-answer instruction raises MMLU-Pro accuracy by 3.8 percentage points at 512 tokens, including +2.7 points when both runs are unfinished. Its gain at 2,048 tokens is uncertain. For gpt-oss, candidate-logit answers from completed low- and medium-effort reasoning are 14.5-26.3 points more accurate than matched-horizon high-effort answers. Most of the 512-token advantage comes from lower effort finishing earlier, while differences among unfinished runs are smaller and mixed. Wrong early answers often concentrate probability on the chosen option, so earlier stopping does not uniformly improve probability quality. In these tests, a tight deadline can favor lower effort or a concise instruction, whereas allowing high effort to finish can recover higher final accuracy. Evaluations should report correct completion before a deadline, the answer obtained when a run is stopped, differences among unfinished runs, and probability assigned to the correct answer separately.

</details>


### [71] [Stop Replacing Noise with Noise: Two-Source Reliability Assessment for Label Correction and Sample Reweighting in Label-Noise Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03432)
*Wenxiao Fan,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，解决基于修补（refurbishment）的噪声标签学习中"源互补"假设的缺陷。传统方法用一个样本级干净度分数同时控制观测标签与伪目标，导致降低对观测标签的信任就自动增加对伪目标的信任。TRACE通过分离评估两路信号（观测标签用损失拟合、浅层关系稳定性、预测一致性；伪目标用模型置信度），实现标签校正与样本重加权解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有refurbishment方法在噪声标签学习中，用一个单一的干净度分数同时控制观测标签分支和伪目标分支，形成隐式耦合：对观测标签信任的降低自动意味着对伪目标信任的提升。这种互补假设是有缺陷的——如果伪目标本身是从受污染的监督中学习而来，它可能复制其试图纠正的噪声，从而导致用另一个不可靠信号替换一个不可靠信号。

Method: 提出TRACE（Two-Source Reliability Assessment）框架：1）通过表征诊断发现噪声监督对深层特征的影响更强，而浅层关系相对稳定；2）对观测标签使用三重评估——损失拟合、浅层关系稳定性、预测一致性；3）对伪目标单独使用模型置信度进行评估；4）用源特定的分数分别控制目标校正（label correction）和监督强度（sample reweighting），而不假设两路可靠性互补。

Result: 在合成噪声基准和真实世界噪声基准上，TRACE改善了代表性的refurbishment基线方法，并产生更可靠的伪监督信号。

Conclusion: 通过解除观测标签与伪目标之间的可靠性互补假设，并利用浅层表征关系作为超越损失后验的额外信息源，TRACE有效缓解了噪声标签学习中"一个不可靠信号替换另一个不可靠信号"的问题，为标签修补方法提供了更稳健的可靠性评估机制。

Abstract: Refurbishment-based noisy-label learning mixes an observed label with a model-derived pseudo target, typically using one sample-wise cleanliness score to control both branches. This creates a hidden coupling: reducing trust in the observed label automatically increases trust in the pseudo target. We show that this complementarity can replace one unreliable signal with another because a pseudo target learned from corrupted supervision may reproduce the noise it is meant to correct. Our representation diagnostics provide a consistent account of this mismatch: noisy supervision redirects deeper layers more strongly, whereas shallower relations remain comparatively stable and provide information beyond the loss posterior. We therefore propose TRACE, a Two-Source Reliability Assessment framework for Label Correction and Sample Reweighting. TRACE assesses the observed label using loss fit, shallow relation stability, and prediction agreement, while separately assessing the pseudo target using model confidence. Its source-specific scores control target correction and supervision strength without assuming complementary reliability. Across synthetic and real-world noisy benchmarks, TRACE improves representative refurbishment baselines and yields more reliable pseudo supervision.

</details>


### [72] [Approximate Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03447)
*Yuannuo Feng,Zegang Peng,Yuxin Xie,Yubing Ye,Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Wenyong Zhou,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出近似投机解码（ASD），一种无需训练的验证器，将传统的二元首次不匹配截断替换为受预算约束的最长前缀选择，通过接受选定的不匹配token并复用后续目标贪心后缀，在不新增草稿模型或微调的情况下提升解码吞吐量。零预算时ASD严格退化为标准贪心验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准投机解码的贪心验证在遇到第一个与目标模型argmax不一致的草稿token时即停止，丢弃后续已被目标模型打分但可能仍可复用的后缀。这种二元截断策略过于保守，限制了验证阶段的吞吐量提升空间。

Method: ASD采用预算化的最长前缀选择机制替代二元首次不匹配截断。具体包括三个核心组件：（1）局部目标logit遗憾门控，用于评估接受不匹配token的代价；（2）每个块内的异常token接受上限；（3）持久化的请求级遗憾预算，从全局角度限制累积误差。ASD接受选定的不匹配token后，直接复用连续的、在实现前缀下仍保持目标贪心的后缀，无需额外的近似决策或额外的目标模型前向传播。该方法无需新草稿模型或微调，预算为零时精确退化为标准贪心验证。

Result: 实验表明，ASD在固定工作负载下相比严格验证将吞吐量提升3.05%–15.26%，在七个Qwen3-14B + DSpark-14B任务上平均提升7.78%。在DeepSeek-V4-Flash (284B) + DSpark的FP4到FP8兼容设置下，GSM8K和MATH-500上的验证器侧接受率提升约10%–16%。

Conclusion: ASD通过灵活的预算化验证策略，在不引入额外训练或模型开销的前提下显著提升了投机解码的验证效率和吞吐量，为大规模自回归解码的性能优化提供了一种实用的替代方案。代码已公开。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by verifying a draft block with a target model in parallel. Under standard greedy verification, decoding stops at the first draft token that differs from the target argmax, discarding the remaining target-scored suffix. Although accepting such a mismatch changes the decoding trajectory, it can make a contiguous suffix reusable when its tokens remain target-greedy under the realized prefix. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{Approximate Speculative Decoding (ASD)}, a training-free verifier that replaces binary first-mismatch truncation with budgeted longest-prefix selection. ASD accepts selected mismatches subject to a local target-logit regret gate, a per-block exception cap, and a persistent request-level regret budget, then reuses the contiguous target-greedy suffix without additional approximate decisions or target-model forward passes. ASD requires neither a new draft model nor fine-tuning, and exactly reduces to standard greedy verification when the budget is zero. Experiments show that ASD improves fixed-workload throughput by $3.05\%$--$15.26\%$ over matched strict verification and averages a $7.78\%$ gain across seven Qwen3-14B + DSpark-14B tasks. On DeepSeek-V4-Flash (284B) with DSpark it also raises verifier-side acceptance by roughly $10\%$--$16\%$ on GSM8K and MATH-500 in an FP4-to-FP8 compatibility setting. The source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/Kissmetothemoon/ASD

</details>


### [73] [Beyond the Gegenbauer Paradigm: q-Orthogonal Kernels for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03482)
*Álvaro Sánchez-Paniagua Ríos,Juan P. Llerena,Alberto Lastra,Nuria Torrado,Edmundo J. Huertas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于离散q-Hermite I多项式的核函数族，将q-正交多项式范式引入SVM核设计。该核在Mercer定理下有效性得到验证，且天然具备有界性以避免湮灭和爆炸效应。在20个基准数据集上的实验表明，该核在数值稳定性和计算简单性方面优于经典核和其他正交多项式核，为量子计算范式提供了概念和算法资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVM性能严重依赖核函数的选择。经典核（如RBF）虽然流行但缺乏可解释性，而正交多项式核可以提供数学上可解释的替代方案并能融入结构化先验知识。现有正交多项式核仍有局限，需要探索更广义的正交多项式族（如q-正交多项式）来提供新思路。

Method: 引入基于离散q-Hermite I多项式的核函数家族，通过变形参数q推广经典Hermite多项式；形式上定义q-Hermite核并证明其满足Mercer定理；利用核的固有有界性避免显式缩放机制；在20个基准数据集上开展对比实验验证性能。

Result: 实验表明q-Hermite核在20个基准数据集上达到与经典核及其他正交多项式核相当的性能，同时在数值稳定性和计算简单性上具有优势。核的天然有界性有效避免了湮灭和爆炸问题。

Conclusion: q-正交多项式构成核设计中的一个有前景方向，将数学优雅性与实际机器学习应用相结合，并为将来的量子计算扩展提供了概念上和算法上的资源。代码已开源在GitHub仓库中以确保完全可复现性。

Abstract: The performance of Support Vector Machines (SVMs) critically depends on the kernel function choice, which enables implicit mapping of data into high-dimensional feature spaces. While classical kernels like Radial Basis Function (RBF) remain popular, orthogonal polynomial kernels offer mathematically interpretable alternatives that can incorporate structured prior knowledge. This work extends the orthogonal polynomial kernel paradigm by introducing a novel family based on discrete $q$-Hermite I polynomials, a class of $q$-orthogonal polynomials that generalize classical Hermite polynomials through a deformation parameter $q$. We formally define the q-Hermite kernel and establish its validity under Mercer's theorem. The kernel's inherent boundedness properties naturally prevent annihilation and explosion effects without requiring explicit scaling mechanisms. Extensive experiments across 20 benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed kernel achieves competitive performance compared to both classical kernels and other orthogonal polynomial kernels, while offering advantages in numerical stability and computational simplicity. Our results confirm that $q$-orthogonal polynomials constitute a promising direction for kernel design, bridging mathematical elegance with practical machine learning applications, that provides conceptual and algorithmic resources that may be further extended to emerging quantum computing paradigms. To facilitate full reproducibility, we provide the complete implementation and experimental pipeline in an open-access GitHub repository at https://github.com/Kokechacho/SVMs-QSVMs.

</details>


### [74] [FedCARE: A Multi-Objective Personalised Federated Learning Framework for Smart Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03498)
*Rojalini Tripathy,Padmalochan Bera,Shreya Ghosh,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedCARE是一个面向智慧医疗的多目标个性化联邦学习框架，采用两阶段训练策略：先基于公共临床特征通过Pareto驱动的多目标联邦优化学习共享全局骨干网络，再由各客户端利用私有特征和本地临床目标进行独立微调，实现机构特异性个性化而无需额外通信开销。在MIMIC-III和Diabetes 130-US Hospitals两个真实医疗数据集上，FedCARE相比FedAvg实现了最高12.5%的AUROC提升和32.0%的MAE降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法在处理真实医疗场景时存在双重局限：现有个性化FL方法主要应对统计异质性（非IID数据），而多目标FL方法通常只学习一个共享全局模型，缺乏客户端级自适应。然而真实医疗联合体不仅包含非IID数据，还涉及异构临床目标和部分重叠的特征空间——不同医院可能优化不同的、甚至相互冲突的临床目标（如死亡风险预测、再入院率降低或住院时长估计），同时保留无法共享的机构特有临床特征。

Method: FedCARE采用两阶段训练策略：(1) 全局学习阶段——从公共临床特征中利用Pareto驱动的多目标联邦优化学习一个共享的全局骨干网络，处理多目标之间的潜在冲突；(2) 个性化微调阶段——每个客户端独立使用其私有特征和本地临床目标对共享骨干进行微调，实现机构特异性个性化且不产生额外通信开销。系统部署在基于云计算的客户端-服务器联邦架构上（墨尔本研究云）。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和Diabetes 130-US Hospitals两个真实医疗数据集上的实验表明，FedCARE持续优于标准FL、多目标FL和个性化FL基线。相比FedAvg，FedCARE取得了最高12.5%的AUROC提升和32.0%的MAE降低。

Conclusion: FedCARE通过将多目标联邦优化与客户端级个性化微调相结合，有效解决了真实医疗联邦场景中统计异质性、目标异构性和特征空间部分重叠共存的复杂问题，验证了该框架在实际云部署环境中的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed healthcare institutions without centralising sensitive patient data. However, real-world healthcare federations are often characterised not only by non-IID data, but also by heterogeneous clinical objectives and partially overlapping feature spaces. Different hospitals may optimise distinct and potentially conflicting objectives, such as mortality risk prediction, readmission reduction, or length-of-stay estimation, while also retaining institution-specific clinical features that cannot be shared with other participants. Existing personalised FL methods mainly address statistical heterogeneity, whereas multi-objective FL approaches typically learn a shared global model without explicit client-level adaptation. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{FedCARE}, a multi-objective personalised FL framework for smart healthcare services. FedCARE follows a two-stage training strategy. First, it learns a shared global backbone from common clinical features using Pareto-driven multi-objective federated optimisation. Second, each client independently fine-tunes the shared backbone using its private features and local clinical objectives, enabling institution-specific personalisation without additional communication overhead. We implement FedCARE in a cloud-based client-server federated deployment on the Melbourne Research Cloud and evaluate it on two real-world healthcare datasets, MIMIC-III and Diabetes 130-US Hospitals. Experimental results show that FedCARE consistently outperforms standard FL, multi-objective FL, and personalised FL baselines, achieving up to 12.5% AUROC improvement and 32.0% MAE reduction over FedAvg.

</details>


### [75] [Robust General Utility for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03562)
*Zixuan Liu,Fangzheng Wu,Brian Summa,Zizhan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出鲁棒一般效用强化学习框架，针对部署时效用函数与训练时不一致的问题，通过minimax优化在效用不确定集内训练鲁棒策略，并开发了凹效用下的投影随机梯度下降-上升算法和非凹效用下的随机prox-extragradient算法，经验证在LLM安全对齐和探索最大化任务中有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统一般效用RL假设效用函数固定且被正确指定，但实际部署时效用可能与训练时存在偏差，产生鲁棒性缺口。之前的work未解决这一问题，因此需要建立对效用错误指定的鲁棒学习框架。

Method: 提出鲁棒一般效用RL的minimax学习框架，将训练策略置于效用不确定集内优化最坏情况下的效用。针对凹效用提出投影随机梯度下降-上升（projected SGD-ascent）方法并建立平稳性保证；针对非凹效用提出随机prox-extragradient算法，缓解非凹性导致的病态行为并保证收敛到近似一阶平稳点。

Result: 理论上前述两种算法分别在不同效用凹性条件下获得收敛保证；实验上在LLM安全对齐和探索最大化任务中验证了收敛行为与理论一致。该框架还统一了标准一般效用RL、奖励鲁棒RL和约束RL等现有框架。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了一般效用RL中效用错误指定鲁棒性的空白，提供了一个可推广的统一框架，并通过理论与实验双重验证了算法的有效性与收敛性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with general utility extends classic RL by optimizing an arbitrary utility functional of the policy-induced occupancy measure, thereby enabling a broader range of applications. However, previous work on general utility RL typically assumes the evaluation utility is fixed and correctly specified. In practice, the utility used at deployment can deviate from the training one, creating a robustness gap that prior work does not address. Motivated by this, we propose robust general-utility RL, a minimax learning framework that trains policies against utility misspecification within a prescribed uncertainty set. Our framework strictly generalizes standard general-utility RL while also providing a unified view of many existing RL frameworks, including reward-robust RL and constrained RL, through appropriate choices of the utility uncertainty set. We further develop provably convergent stochastic algorithms for two regimes. For concave utilities, we develop a projected stochastic gradient descent-ascent method and establish stationarity guarantees. For the more challenging nonconcave regime, we propose a stochastic prox-extragradient algorithm that mitigates ill-posed behavior induced by nonconcavity, with convergence guarantees to approximate first-order stationarity. Experiments on LLM safety alignment and exploration maximization tasks further corroborate the convergence behavior consistent with our theory.

</details>


### [76] [Pin Once, Swap Light: Subspace-Aligned Centroid-Residual Training for Efficient Ultra-LoRA Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03579)
*Xiang Li,Pengcheng Wang,Huazheng Wang,Saurabh Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SALT是一个面向多租户LoRA服务系统的效率感知分层微调框架，通过联合训练域质心（domain centroid）并让用户在其上微调超低秩残差适配器（r≤2），在不牺牲任务性能的前提下大幅降低GPU显存占用和PCIe交换开销，实现高效的多LoRA并发服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多租户LoRA服务系统面临一个关键的系统性困境：高秩适配器任务性能好但显存占用大、PCIe交换开销高，严重制约可扩展性；而超低秩适配器（r≤2）虽能最小化显存和PCIe传输开销，却导致下游任务性能下降。现有方法无法同时兼顾服务效率和任务性能。

Method: SALT采用三阶段分层微调框架：(1) 提供商在公共域数据上联合训练高容量域质心（domain centroid），使用新颖的对齐正则化器（alignment regularizer）将域内任务子空间聚合并统一到同一基空间；(2) 用户在冻结的质心之上，仅微调超低秩（r≤2）任务残差适配器；(3) 推理阶段，提供商将质心固定在GPU VRAM中，按需动态交换每个用户的任务残差。

Result: 在多种规模的LLM上，SALT使用r≤2的超低秩残差即可恢复高秩精度，相比最先进压缩基线实现了最高18.5%的绝对精度提升，并将每适配器内存减少最多16倍。集成到vLLM后，在Llama-3.2-3B上，PCIe带宽受限场景下服务吞吐量提升达51%，GPU VRAM受限场景下吞吐量提升达28%。

Conclusion: SALT通过将高容量域知识沉淀到共享质心中，使得超低秩任务残差足以编码任务特有信息，从而在保持高任务性能的同时显著降低系统资源开销，有效解决了多租户LoRA服务中效率与性能之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Modern multi-tenant Low-Rank Adapters (LoRAs) serving systems concurrently host tens to hundreds of LoRA adapters. Though powerful, this introduces a critical system dilemma between serving efficiency and task performance: higher-rank adapters generally achieve better downstream task performance, but their GPU VRAM footprint and Host-to-Device PCIe swapping overhead severely constrain scalability. Conversely, ultra-low-rank adapters ($r \le 2$) minimize both VRAM footprint and PCIe transfer overhead, but suffer from downstream task performance degradation. To solve this problem, we propose Subspace-Aligned LoRA Training (SALT), a serving efficiency-aware hierarchical fine-tuning framework. Our solution operates in three phases. First, a provider jointly trains high-capacity domain centroids on public data within the domain using a novel alignment regularizer that coheres in-domain task subspaces into a unified basis. Next, users fine-tune ultra-low-rank task residual adapters on private data atop those frozen centroids. Finally, during inference, the provider pins the centroid in GPU VRAM and dynamically swaps in each user's task residual on demand. Across LLMs of varying scales, SALT recovers high-rank accuracy using $r \le 2$ residuals, achieving up to 18.5% absolute accuracy gains over state-of-the-art compression baselines and reducing per-adapter memory by up to 16x. When integrated into vLLM, SALT improves serving throughput by up to 51% under PCIe bandwidth pressure and 28% under GPU VRAM constraints for Llama-3.2-3B.

</details>


### [77] [Design-Time Optimization of Deep Neural Networks for Intermittent Learning on Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03589)
*Jakob Schubert,Maximilian Kasper,Maximilian Linke,Benedict Herzog,Mark Deutel,Axel Plinge,Dominik Seuss,Christopher Mutschler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于微控制器（MCU）上间歇性能量自主设备端学习的深度神经网络（DNN）设计方法。通过结合硬件感知的能量预测模型与多目标优化（MOO），在离线设计阶段优化DNN架构，无需在目标MCU上反复部署和在线测试。能量预测器可估算每个层的推理和训练能耗（包括间歇性检查点开销），在Cortex-M4 MCU上使用自编码器进行异常检测验证，预测加权绝对百分比误差为16.6%，足以在间歇性约束下进行可靠的架构选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在太阳能等能量受限的移动应用中，能量随时可能耗尽，导致设备端AI学习过程随时被打断。现有的DNN设计方法缺乏对间歇性能源约束下训练能耗的考虑，需要一种在设计阶段就能预测和优化DNN架构的方法，以避免重复部署和在线测试的高昂成本。

Method: 将硬件感知的能量预测模型与多目标优化（MOO）相结合。能量预测器基于DNN模型的实现特定计算和内存特征，逐层估算推理和训练能耗，并包含间歇性检查点开销。通过离线优化实现DNN架构的自动设计，无需在目标MCU上反复部署测试。

Result: 在Cortex-M4 MCU上使用自编码器进行异常检测验证，能量预测器实现了16.6%的加权绝对百分比误差（WAPE），该精度足以在间歇性约束下进行可靠的架构选择。

Conclusion: 这项工作弥合了多目标优化、自动化DNN设计、能量收集系统部署和间歇性学习之间的鸿沟，真正实现了边缘端自主AI。证明了离线设计阶段进行能量感知的DNN优化是可行且有效的。

Abstract: We present a method for designing deep neural networks (DNNs) for intermittent, energy-autonomous, on-device learning on microcontroller units (MCUs). In mobile applications where the energy can run out, e.g., when solar-powered, executing artificial intelligence (AI) faces a technical issue as learning can be interrupted at any time. Our approach combines a hardware-aware energy prediction model with multi-objective optimization (MOO), enabling offline DNN optimization at the design stage without repeated deployment and online testing on the target MCU. Our proposed energy predictor estimates per-layer energy consumption for both DNN inference and training, including the intermittent checkpointing overhead, based on implementation-specific compute and memory features extracted from the DNN model. We validate our approach using autoencoders for anomaly detection on a Cortex-M4 MCU, where our predictor achieves a weighted absolute percentage error of 16.6%, which is sufficient for reliable architecture selection under intermittency constraints. As a result, this work bridges the gap between MOO, automated DNN design, deployment on energy-harvesting systems, and intermittent learning, truly enabling autonomous AI at the edge.

</details>


### [78] [A Theory of Conditional Collapse under Low-Rank Weight-Space Ablations: I. The Single-Block Theory and Synthetic Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03620)
*Abdallah Khemais*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在残差流条件计算的理论框架下，严格分析了激活补丁（activation patching）与权重空间消融（weight-space ablation）何时在因果归因上达成一致，推导出三个精确结果，并通过合成任务的小型transformer实验验证了所有预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活补丁和权重消融都被用来声称某组件对行为有因果责任，但两者操作的对象不同——一个是单次前向传播中的激活值，另一个是影响每一次前向传播的权重参数。理解二者何时一致是因果归因方法可靠性的核心问题，但目前缺乏严格的理论分析。

Method: 作者构建了一个理想化模型，其中条件计算通过残差流加性展开：F(x)=F_0(x)+Σ_i α_i(x)v_i，由线性泛函读出。在此框架下推导了三组精确结果：1) 删除载体子集使匹配输入对坍缩到相同输出的充要条件（对称性与无外部对比），并给出近似的确定性误差形式；2) 补丁移动读出值至供体-受体对比度，消融移动至绝对电平，二者互不约束，构造了单载体补丁全部翻转决策而消融均不翻转的实例；3) 对注意力头与其归一化和MLP的组合，推导出精确的一阶交互公式并给出可证明的二阶余项，当仅消融MLP时余项恒为零而消融注意力头时一般不为零。

Result: 在合成条件任务上训练的小型transformer验证了三组预测：39种消融配置下，测得的交互与理想模型的预测精度强秩相关（Spearman -0.83）；第二项任务和不同的架构复现了相同模式，包括极性反转。单块交互结果可推广到多个残差块。

Conclusion: 激活补丁与权重消融在理论上存在系统性差异：补丁反映的是供体-受体对比，而消融反映的是绝对电平，二者可能完全不一致。本文提供了精确的条件刻画何时二者一致，以及何时会出现极端分歧（如补丁全翻转而消融不翻转）。这些理论预测在合成实验中得到验证，并将在配套论文中扩展到真实预训练模型。

Abstract: Activation patching and weight-space ablation both claim a component is causally responsible for a behavior, yet they act on different objects: one forward pass versus the parameters behind every forward pass. We ask when they agree.
  We study an idealized model where a conditional computation is carried additively through a residual stream, $F(x)=F_0(x)+\sum_iα_i(x)v_i$, read out by a linear functional, and prove three exact results. First, deleting a subset of carriers collapses a matched input pair onto the same unconditional output \emph{if and only if} the removal is symmetric on the pair and leaves no outside contrast; the error is deterministic, and we give its exact form even when the two conditions hold only approximately. Second, patching a carrier moves the readout by its donor-receiver \emph{contrast}, while ablating it moves the readout by its \emph{absolute level}; neither bounds the other, and we construct pairs where every single-carrier patch flips the decision while no single-carrier ablation does. Third, for an attention head composed with its own layer's normalization and MLP, we derive an exact first-order interaction formula with a provably second-order remainder, vanishing identically when only the MLP is ablated but not, in general, when a head is.
  Small transformers trained on a synthetic conditional task illustrate all three predictions: across thirty-nine ablation configurations the measured interaction is strongly rank-correlated with the idealized model's predictive accuracy (Spearman $-0.83$), and a second task and architecture reproduces the same pattern, including a further polarity reversal.
  The single-block interaction result extends past one residual block, and the synthetic validation is tested against a real pretrained model, in a companion paper that takes this theory further along both axes.

</details>


### [79] [ConformalShift: Targeted Event Reordering Against Adaptive ECG Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03628)
*Arash Vashagh,Yasmin Vashagh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ConformalShift，一种针对自适应共形预测监控系统的有界事件重排攻击，通过仅改变真实事件的时间顺序来抑制心室类别预测，而无需修改心电图波形、标签、分类器分数或事件集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应共形预测可以恢复点分类器遗漏的临床重要心跳类别，但其决策对事件顺序敏感，因为延迟反馈机制使预测依赖于事件到达的时间顺序。这种敏感性可能被利用来破坏医疗监控系统的可靠性，而现有工作未系统研究此类基于时序信息的攻击。

Method: ConformalShift通过搜索合法前序事件的可行排列（满足有界位移约束），在目标事件评估之前降低心室类别的阈值，从而抑制心室类预测。该攻击不修改波形、标签、分类器分数或事件多重集合，仅操纵事件的到达顺序。

Result: 在独立的MIT-BIH确认记录上，该攻击对Extra Trees抑制了66.7%的合格目标（随机调度基线为4.4%），对HistGradientBoosting抑制了60.0%（随机基线为12.0%）。迁移配置在INCART上仍优于随机调度，而减少位移预算会削弱两个数据集上的攻击效果。

Conclusion: 研究证明，即使波形、标签、分类器输出和事件内容均保持不变，仅通过操控真实信息的到达时间，自适应监控系统在医疗健康场景中也可能被恶意破坏，揭示了一种新型的时序侧信道攻击面。

Abstract: Adaptive conformal prediction can recover clinically important heartbeat classes missed by a point classifier, but delayed feedback makes its decisions sensitive to event order. We introduce ConformalShift, a bounded event-reordering attack that suppresses the ventricular class for rescued events without modifying ECG waveforms, labels, classifier scores, or the event multiset. ConformalShift searches for feasible permutations of authentic preceding events that lower the ventricular threshold before a selected target is evaluated. On disjoint MIT--BIH confirmation records, the attack suppressed 66.7% of eligible targets for Extra Trees and 60.0% for HistGradientBoosting, compared with random-schedule rates of 4.4% and 12.0%, respectively. Transferred configurations also outperformed random scheduling on INCART, while reducing the displacement budget weakened the attack on both datasets. These results show that adaptive monitors in healthcare can be compromised through the timing of authentic information, even when waveforms, labels, classifier outputs, and event contents remain unchanged.

</details>


### [80] [POEM: Phase-Aware $\mathrm{SO}(2)$ Feature Rotation for Time Series Forecasting Under Periodicity Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03630)
*Jiawen Zhu,Shuhan Liu,Shengxuan Li,Qiming Shi,Di Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出POEM，一种基于SO(2)群潜在特征旋转的相位感知时间序列预测框架，通过学习相位校正坐标并应用可逆旋转来减轻周期性漂移问题，同时利用方向性相位增量注意力（DPIA）从历史中检索相似上下文来外推相位校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测中的周期性漂移（周期时序和相位随时间变化）仍是一个挑战性问题。现有方法主要在固定时间网格上建模序列，难以适应相位相关的变化，缺乏处理相位变异的能力。

Method: 提出POEM框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 利用二维特殊正交群SO(2)对配对潜在特征施加可逆旋转，学习相位校正坐标以降低相位相关变异性；(2) 方向性相位增量注意力（DPIA）从相似时间上下文中检索历史相位增量，并将其外推至未来相位校正。

Result: 实验表明POEM取得了有竞争力的性能，定性可视化显示所学的相位感知变换使潜在轨迹更加规则化。

Conclusion: 通过SO(2)旋转机制和DPIA注意力机制，POEM有效地解决了周期性漂移问题，在预测性能上达到了竞争水平，且学习到的相位感知变换有助于提升潜在表示的规律性。

Abstract: Deep learning has advanced time series forecasting, but periodicity drift, in which cycle timing and phase vary over time, remains a challenging problem. Existing methods predominantly model these sequences on fixed time grids, suffering from a limited ability to accommodate phase-related variation. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{POEM}, a phase-aware forecasting framework based on latent feature rotation using the special orthogonal group in two dimensions, denoted by $\mathrm{SO}(2)$. POEM aims to reduce the phase-related variability by learning a phase-correction coordinate and applying an invertible $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-based rotation to paired latent features. To extrapolate this correction coordinate, Directional Phase Increment Attention (DPIA) retrieves historical phase increments from similar temporal contexts and integrates them into future phase corrections. Experiments demonstrate that POEM achieves competitive performance, while qualitative visualizations suggest that the learned phase-aware transformation makes latent trajectories more regular.

</details>


### [81] [CausalOPD: First-Wrong-Step Supervision for Distilling Causal Chain Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03673)
*Jian Zhang,Bingyi Wang,Yizhi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalOPD框架，一种面向因果链推理任务的课程式在线过程蒸馏方法。通过知识增强教师提供基于领域因果规则的轨迹，学生在自身的rollout分布上生成轨迹并识别首个错误步骤，从已验证前缀出发进行短视距强化学习修复局部故障，并按因果阶段课程（证据级→机制级→结论级）递进训练。在三个领域上，路径正确率平均提升23.4个百分点，右标签错推理率从15.7%降至4.4%，且8B学生模型在路径正确率上超越两个专有参考系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准轨迹模仿学习无法纠正学生自身rollout分布上的过程错误。在临床诊断、法律判决、工业故障诊断等需要逐步因果链的推理任务中，早期错误会传播放大，且模型可能掩盖错误推理得到正确结论。同时，隐私、延迟和可控性需求促使将大语言模型蒸馏到本地可部署的小模型中，但现有蒸馏方法缺乏对推理过程正确性的保障。

Method: CausalOPD包含四个核心组件：(1)知识增强教师：利用领域因果规则、实体关系和结构约束生成确定性轨迹；(2)首个错误步骤定位：教师识别学生rollout轨迹中最早违反可用约束的转移；(3)短视距强化学习：从已验证前缀出发，通过短视野RL精准修复局部失败而非整个轨迹；(4)因果阶段课程：按传播顺序组织课程，从证据级到机制级再到结论级错误逐步递进训练。

Result: 在三个因果推理领域（临床诊断、法律判断、工业故障诊断）中，CausalOPD相比序列级在线过程蒸馏，路径正确率平均提高23.4个百分点，右标签错推理率从15.7%降至4.4%。领域特化的8B学生模型在路径正确率上全面超越两个专有参考模型。

Conclusion: 通过首次错误步骤的精准定位与修复，结合因果传播顺序的课程设计，CausalOPD有效解决了在线过程蒸馏中学生分布偏移问题，实现了高保真的因果推理能力蒸馏到小模型，显著降低了"答案对但推理错"的风险。

Abstract: Many critical reasoning tasks, including clinical diagnosis, legal judgment, and industrial fault diagnosis, require step-dependent causal chains in which early errors propagate and correct conclusions can mask invalid reasoning. Although large language models perform well on such tasks, privacy, latency, and controllability motivate distillation into locally deployable models. Standard trajectory imitation does not correct process errors on the student's own rollout distribution. We propose CausalOPD, a curriculum online process distillation framework. A knowledge-augmented teacher first provides trajectories grounded in domain-specific causal rules, entity relations, and structural constraints. The student then generates on-policy trajectories, and the teacher identifies the first wrong step, defined as the earliest transition that verifiably violates available constraints. Starting from the verified prefix, short-horizon reinforcement learning repairs this localized failure. A causal-stage curriculum advances from evidence-level to mechanism-level and conclusion-level errors, following their propagation order. Across three domains, CausalOPD improves average path correctness by 23.4 percentage points over sequence-level online process distillation and reduces the right-label-wrong-reasoning rate from 15.7% to 4.4%. The domain-specific 8B students also surpass both evaluated proprietary references in path correctness across all domains.

</details>


### [82] [DiagLoop: A Counterfactual Data Flywheel with Stage-Localized Reinforcement for Diagnostic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03674)
*Jian Zhang,Bingyi Wang,Yizhi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiagLoop，一个反事实数据飞轮框架，通过将物理关系或临床指南编码为训练监督，仅使用合成场景即可训练出8B规模的因果诊断模型。该模型在八个工业系统和十个疾病类别上的严格路径正确性分别比最强传统基线提升11.6和5.5个点，并超越所评估的专有参考模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果诊断模型必须解释结论如何从证据中得出（因为诊断指导维修和治疗），但严重案例稀缺、记录通常缺乏推理路径、数据在不同配置间迁移效果差，这都使得本地部署困难。

Method: DiagLoop采用训练专用的教师-学生架构：教师基于每个机制家族一次性编写的物理关系或临床指南，通过变化原因、情境和观察来提出反事实世界；独立的混合检查器仅接收有效世界。学生依次进行症状抽象、因果链构建和根因归因，并依据阶段特定标准识别最早失败点。对于非终止性失败，采用有界修复探测下游能力，所得弱点画像指导后续数据生成。阶段局部强化学习仅更新模型生成的延续部分，同时使用回放和保留机制减少遗忘。

Result: 仅使用合成场景且无需案例级专家推理标注，8B模型在八个工业系统上严格路径正确性提升11.6点，在十个疾病类别上提升5.5点；相对于混乱路由对照分别提升3.9和2.3点；在两个领域均超过评估的专有参考模型（即使参考模型获得少样本示例或上下文规范）。

Conclusion: 将机制知识编码为反事实数据生成循环，可以在没有真实案例标注的情况下有效训练因果诊断模型，且该框架在不同配置间具有良好的泛化能力，为本地部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Causal diagnostic models must explain how conclusions follow from evidence because diagnoses guide repairs and treatments. Yet serious cases are scarce, records rarely contain reasoning paths, and data transfer poorly across configurations, complicating local deployment. We present DiagLoop, a counterfactual data flywheel that converts codified physical relations or clinical guidelines, authored once per mechanism family, into training supervision beyond recorded cases. A training-only teacher proposes counterfactual worlds by varying causes, contexts, and observations, while an independent hybrid checker admits only valid worlds. The student reasons through symptom abstraction, causal-chain construction, and root-cause attribution. Stage-specific criteria identify its earliest failure. For nonterminal failures, a bounded repair probes downstream competence, and the resulting weakness profile guides subsequent data generation. Stage-localized reinforcement learning updates only the model-generated continuation, while replay and preservation reduce forgetting. The same criteria govern admission, attribution, reward, and regeneration through checks separate from the proposer. Using only synthesized scenarios and no case-level expert reasoning annotations, the resulting 8B model improves strict path correctness over the strongest conventional baseline. Gains are 11.6 points across eight industrial systems and 5.5 points across ten disease categories. Gains over a deranged-routing control are 3.9 and 2.3 points, respectively. The model also exceeds the evaluated proprietary references in both domains, even when they receive few-shot examples or the specification in context.

</details>


### [83] [LAEF: A Lead-Agnostic ECG Foundation Model Towards Point-of-Care Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03690)
*Edoardo Coppola,Stefano Fiorini,Pietro Liò,Mattia Savardi,Alberto Signoroni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LAEF是一个7M参数的导联无关心电图基础模型，将ECG表示为可变大小的时空图，通过图注意力网络原生处理任意导联子集（无需零填充）。在9.2M条12导联ECG上通过掩码节点建模和随机导联采样预训练，在下游18个数据集中表现优于零填充替代方案，尤其在1-2导联的点诊场景下平均AUROC提升+3.2点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备和手持ECG记录仪通常仅采集1-2个导联，但现有ECG基础模型架构上被限定为12导联输入，在导联减少时会性能下降甚至失效。需要一个能够原生适应任意导联子集的ECG基础模型。

Method: 将ECG建模为可变规模的时空图，包含基于生理学动机的导联内和导联间连接，由Graph Attention Network处理，能够随活跃导联数自然扩展。采用掩码节点建模和随机导联采样策略，在9.2M条12导联ECG上进行预训练。

Result: 在完整12导联下与12倍大的专用基线持平；在1-2导联的点诊诊断场景下，单随机导联时17/18数据集、2导联时14/18数据集上优于所有零填充替代方案，平均AUROC增益+3.2点。表示分析将优势归因于架构上的导联无关性；对164种心血管疾病的导联重要性研究表明，单标准导联下群体水平性能稳定，同时保留已确立的临床导联-疾病关联。

Conclusion: LAEF通过架构层面的导联无关设计，实现了从12导联临床场景到1-2导联可穿戴点诊场景的无缝迁移，无需任何架构修改或零填充，为多导联ECG基础模型提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Point-of-care cardiac devices such as smartwatches and handheld ECG recorders typically capture 1--2 leads, yet existing ECG foundation models are architecturally constrained to fixed 12-lead inputs, degrading or failing under these reduced configurations. We introduce LAEF (Lead-Agnostic ECG Foundation), a 7M-parameter ECG foundation model that can natively process any lead subset without zero-padding or architectural modification. LAEF represents ECGs as variable-size spatiotemporal graphs with physiologically motivated intra- and inter-lead connectivity, processed by a Graph Attention Network that scales naturally with active lead count.Pre-trained on 9.2M 12-lead ECGs via masked node modelling with stochastic lead sampling, LAEF learns representations robust to lead configuration. Across 18 downstream datasets, LAEF is on par with specialized 12-lead baselines over 12$\times$ larger at full lead availability. Under direct point-of-care-oriented diagnostics (1--2 leads), it outperforms all zero-padded alternatives on 17 out of 18 datasets with with a single randomly sampled lead and on 14 out of 18 with 2 leads, with an average AUROC gain of +3.2 points. Representation analysis links this advantage to architectural lead-agnosticism, and a lead-importance study across 164 cardiovascular conditions shows population-level performance is stable across single standard input leads while still recovering established clinically lead-condition associations.

</details>


### [84] [Learning and Clustering on Temporal Graphs: Principles, Primitives, and Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03696)
*Nelson Aloysio Reis de Almeida Passos,Emanuele Carlini,Salvatore Trani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究围绕时序图上的聚类任务展开，从原理（谱基础与可检测性阈值）、原语（GPU加速的谱聚类与多切片模块度优化）和池化（社区检测作为时序图的粗粒化算子）三个视角，系统比较了算法方法（谱聚类、模块度优化）与图神经网络在时序图聚类上的适用边界，发现属性信号弱时算法方法更具优势，而信号对齐时神经模型更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管图神经网络在下游图任务中达到最先进性能，但在聚类任务上，其相对传统描述性和推断性聚类算法的优势尚不明确，尤其在效率和恢复精度要求下。作者试图通过统一的框架（原理、原语、池化）来澄清这一张力的本质。

Method: 从三个相互关联的视角展开研究：(1) 原理层面：通过共享的谱基础和随机块模型（SBM）中的可检测性阈值，将图学习和社区检测联系起来；(2) 原语层面：通过GPU加速的时序后端使谱聚类和多切片模块度优化变得可计算；(3) 池化层面：将原理驱动的社区检测视为时序图上具有理论基础的粗粒化算子。

Result: 结果表明：(1) 在属性缺失或较弱的情况下，算法方法（谱聚类、模块度优化）仍是更合适的选择，其瓶颈在于可扩展性而非精度；(2) 当结构、时序和属性信号对齐时，神经模型最有说服力；(3) GPU加速的原语使时序聚类可扩展，为理论驱动的池化提供了可行路径，但引出了一个核心问题：基于社区检测的粗粒化在何种条件下能保留下游学习任务所需的动态信息。

Conclusion: 算法方法（谱聚类、模块度优化）在属性信号弱时仍应作为首选工具，其核心障碍是可扩展性而非准确性；神经模型在多种信号对齐时才更有优势。通过GPU加速使社区检测方法可扩展，为理论驱动的时序图池化提供了新的可能，但需要进一步研究社区级粗粒化对下游动态信号保真度的影响。

Abstract: This work focuses on the problem of learning on temporal graphs, with particular emphasis on the task of clustering: obtaining coarse-grained representations by aggregating information from nodes, edges, and temporal dynamics - a task related to pooling in machine learning on graphs, or community detection in network science. Although graph neural networks reach state-of-the-art performance across many downstream graph tasks, their advantage over established descriptive and inferential clustering algorithms is far less settled, especially under demands of efficiency and recovery accuracy. We frame this tension through three linked perspectives: principles, connecting graph learning and community detection through shared spectral foundations and detectability thresholds in stochastic block model regimes; primitives, making spectral clustering and multislice modularity optimization tractable through GPU-accelerated temporal backends; and pooling, viewing principled community detection as a theory-grounded coarse-graining operator for temporal graphs. Our results indicate that algorithmic methods remain the appropriate tool where attributes are absent or weak - scalability rather than accuracy being the binding obstacle - while neural models are most compelling when structural, temporal, and attribute signals align. By making temporal clustering scalable, GPU-accelerated primitives suggest a route toward theory-grounded pooling, while raising a central question: when does community-based coarse-graining preserve the dynamics needed for downstream learning tasks?

</details>


### [85] [To Describe or Construct Statistical Learning Models Using the Category-theoretical Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03706)
*Congwei Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一份综述性报告，面向初学者总结经典统计学习模型和著名算法，并从范畴论（category theory）视角提供对统计学习模型的理解框架，旨在吸引包括基础数学在内的其他领域研究者参与统计学习研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计学习是机器学习/人工智能的主流领域，成果丰硕，但作者希望引入范畴论这一新的理论视角来理解统计学习模型，从而吸引更多来自基础数学等领域的跨学科研究者参与统计学习相关研究，拓展该领域的研究深度和广度。

Method: 作者首先系统性地总结了若干经典统计学习模型与知名算法（适合初学者阅读），然后引入范畴论作为抽象工具，从范畴论视角重新审视和构建对统计学习模型的理解框架。

Result: 该报告提供了一份面向初学者的统计学习基础知识总结，并提出了一个基于范畴论的统计学习模型理解视角，为跨学科研究者（特别是基础数学家）进入该领域提供了桥梁。

Conclusion: 作者希望通过范畴论视角的报告吸引其他领域（尤其是基础数学）的研究者参与统计学习研究，促进跨学科融合并激发新的研究方向。

Abstract: Statistical learning is a fascinating field that has long been the mainstream of machine learning/artificial intelligence. A large number of results have been produced which can be widely applied to real-world problems. It also leads to many research topics and also stimulates new research. This report summarizes some classical statistical learning models and well-known algorithms, especially for amateurs, and provides a category-theoretic perspective on understanding statistical learning models. The aim is to attract researchers from other fields, including basic mathematics, to participate in the research related to statistical learning.

</details>


### [86] [Amortized Interventional Forecasting for Multivariate CIR Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03715)
*Andreas Sauter,Sumit Sourabh,Drona Kandhai,Erman Acar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CIR-ACTIVA框架，用于估计多元平均回归时间序列（如信用违约互换利差）中的分布因果效应。该框架包含一个摊销模型来预测多时间尺度下的冲击响应，以及一个因果多元CIR数据生成过程来提供成对的观测与干预地面真值。在CDS利差测试平台上验证，该框架在因果选择性和校准方面优于观测与摊销因果推断基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CIR过程虽广泛用于金融时间序列建模（从短期利率到CDS利差），但只能捕捉序列间的相关共动，无法回答外部冲击下一个序列对系统的因果影响。观测条件分布会与历史共动混淆，无法反映系统的真实冲击响应，因此需要一种能够估计分布因果效应的方法。

Method: (1) 提出摊销模型，将轨迹视为带时间戳的观测，预测校准后的多时间尺度冲击响应，无需按场景重新训练；(2) 构建因果多元CIR数据生成过程，提供成对的观测与干预地面真值；(3) 使用CDS利差作为测试平台进行实例化与校准。

Result: 在合成数据上验证了CIR-ACTIVA的有效性，并在真实CDS数据上通过回溯测试评估其实践基础。与观测和摊销因果推断基线相比，该方法在联合分布中的因果选择性和按时间尺度校准方面均领先，且在干预分布随时间尺度变化时仍能保持选择性，增益集中在短时间尺度。

Conclusion: CIR-ACTIVA开辟了一类关于耦合利差系统的what-if查询，包括CDS压力测试，这些是纯观测预测器无法回答的问题。

Abstract: Mean-reverting dynamics are pervasive in finance, and the Cox--Ingersoll--Ross (CIR) process is a standard model for the time series they produce, from short rates to credit default swap (CDS) spreads. Yet CIR models capture only \emph{correlated} co-movement, not \emph{causal} influence between series, so they cannot answer the system's response when one series is externally shocked, which observational conditionals confound with historical co-movement. We make two contributions. First, an amortized model for distributional causal effect estimation that frames trajectories as time-stamped observations and predicts the calibrated multi-horizon shock response without retraining per scenario. Second, a causal multivariate CIR data-generating process that supplies the paired observational and interventional ground truth that real markets cannot. We instantiate and calibrate the framework on CDS spreads as a testbed. CIR-ACTIVA's validity is established on synthetic ground truth, independent of how well the simulator matches reality, while practical grounding is assessed by backtesting the generated traces against real CDS data. Against observational and amortized causal-inference baselines, CIR-ACTIVA leads on both causal selectivity in the joint distribution and horizon-resolved calibration, retaining its selectivity once the interventional law varies over the horizon, with gains concentrating at short horizons. This opens up a class of what-if queries on coupled spread systems, CDS stress testing among them, that observational forecasters cannot answer.

</details>


### [87] [UNVaMP: Neural Knowledge Tracing with Variational Regularization of Latent Knowledge Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03811)
*Carson J. Cook,Ahmed J. Zerouali,Anthony Schmidt,Reginald Ziedzor,Paul Lin,Luke G. Eglington*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UNVaMP架构，一种结合学生-项目交互与内部记忆的知识追踪方法，生成动态潜在知识表征。纯神经配置（UNVaMP-MLP）在四个数据集的三个中取得最佳预测性能，而混合配置（UNVaMP-MIRT）预测性能仅略逊，解释了可解释性的微小代价，并提供了波动性控制、不确定性量化及灵活输入等额外能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识追踪任务需要在预测学生未来表现的同时估计其知识状态，但现有方法往往难以同时兼顾预测准确性和可解释性，也缺乏对学习轨迹平滑性的显式控制以及对知识状态估计不确定性的量化。

Method: UNVaMP采用统一神经变分框架，将学生-项目交互与内部记忆机制融合，产生演化的潜在知识表征。架构可配置为纯神经模型（UNVaMP-MLP）或混合模型（UNVaMP-MIRT，使用1PL MIRT测量函数），后者通过可解释的测量函数在潜在空间上进行响应预测。

Result: UNVaMP-MLP在四个数据集的三个上取得最强预测性能；UNVaMP-MIRT性能仅略逊，表明可解释性的预测代价较小。实验还表明辅助输入能引发其预测行为的结构化变化，仿真研究验证其在受控测量条件下能产生稳健的知识状态估计。

Conclusion: UNVaMP兼具实用性和结构性恢复能力，既可服务于真实教育系统，又能从学生-项目交互中恢复潜在结构，表明其在预测性能、可解释性和方法学特性之间达到了良好平衡。

Abstract: We introduce the Unified Neural Variational Measurement of Proficiency (UNVaMP) architecture, a knowledge tracing method that integrates observed student-item interactions with internal memory to produce evolving latent representations of student knowledge. These representations support accurate predictions of future responses while enabling explicit control over the smoothness of estimated learning trajectories. UNVaMP can be configured as either a purely neural model or a hybrid model that predicts responses through an interpretable measurement function over the latent space. We show that a pure neural configuration (UNVaMP-MLP) achieves the strongest predictive performance among compared models on three out of four datasets. Meanwhile, a hybrid configuration (UNVaMP-MIRT, using a 1PL MIRT measurement function) lags only slightly behind UNVaMP-MLP, indicating that the predictive cost of interpretability is modest.
  Beyond predictive accuracy, UNVaMP provides the following: a principled mechanism for controlling volatility when estimating student latent variables, quantification of uncertainty over student knowledge state estimates, and flexible input specification that supports heterogeneous student-item interaction features. In addition, the hybrid UNVaMP-MIRT configuration generates interpretable moment-in-time student knowledge state estimates. Using an experimental dataset, we show that auxiliary inputs induce structured changes in the predictive behavior of UNVaMP-MIRT, consistent with sensitivity to underlying structure beyond response correctness. Furthermore, through a simulation study, we show that UNVaMP yields well-behaved knowledge state estimates under controlled measurement conditions. In total, these results indicate that UNVaMP is both useful for real-world education systems and capable of recovering underlying structure from student-item interactions.

</details>


### [88] [Resume Means Resume: A Machine-Checked Conformance Contract for Checkpoint, Interrupt, and Resume Semantics in Workflow Persistence Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03836)
*Sajjad Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定义了RESUME CONTRACT（六条持久化API性质加fork-intent与活锁义务），用TLA+模型以7.4百万状态规模穷举校验参考语义，并用39格故障矩阵分析框架行为。实测五个主流Agent工作流框架均无机器可检查契约且行为违规：LangGraph跨崩溃是at-least-once、CrewAI重放效应方法、pydantic-graph崩溃后无法恢复，且并发下消费一次性质失效（k进程触发k次效应）。作者提出REMIT参考序列器，其Verus验证恢复核心与发布可执行文件逐行一致，修复fork与有效性故障格，并通过共享存储的opt-in门禁实现跨进程消费一次修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent工作流框架需要持久化执行状态以支持中断恢复和崩溃恢复，但恢复语义（尤其是已触发效应的处理）在不同框架间定义不一、无机器可检查契约，且行为违反其声称的保证，缺乏系统性分析与可验证修复方案。

Method: 定义RESUME CONTRACT（六条性质：前缀延续、效应恰好一次、fork确定性、检查点有效性、消费一次、恢复确定性，加fork意图与活络义务）；用TLA+对参考语义进行穷举模型检查（规模扩展至7.4百万状态）；构造39格故障矩阵定位各框架的分离行为；设计确定性、无LLM的测试基准在固定版本上测量；实现REMIT参考序列器，其恢复核心经Verus形式化验证且与发布可执行文件逐行一致。

Result: 五个探针框架无共享一致性画像；LangGraph 1.2.9持久化第二个恢复值但从不读取、静默持久化schema非法状态、真实SIGKILL后重放已持久化工作（同一API上中断是恰好一次、崩溃是至少一次）；CrewAI 1.15.2违反其书面声明重放带效应的已执行方法；pydantic-graph 1.x在节点中途崩溃后无法恢复。消费一次性质在顺序条件下成立但并发交付下失败：k进程恢复同一停驻中断会触发k次门控效应，40格中36格饱和度为1.0，且跨主机传播。REMIT修复fork与有效性格；跨进程格在读路径修复，opt-in门禁在共享存储中声明消费，仅服务一个竞争进程并拒绝其余，且该系统实际发布。

Conclusion: 恢复语义的正确性必须精确到崩溃与并发的交叉点，不能依赖非机器可检查的文档声明；形式化验证与参考实现相结合的"验证核心逐行等同发布代码"方法可行；跨进程消费一次可通过共享存储上的原子认领（opt-in门禁）实现，且该修复方案能够实际部署。

Abstract: A framework that persists execution state so a run can be interrupted, survive a crash, and continue must decide what a resume means for effects that already fired. Five widely deployed agent workflow frameworks answer differently, none exposes a machine-checkable contract, and behavior violates even the fragments they state. The RESUME CONTRACT states six properties over the persistence API (prefix continuation, effect exactly-once, fork determinism, checkpoint validity, consume-once, recovery determinism), plus fork-intent and liveness obligations. A TLA+ model checks a reference semantics exhaustively, unchanged at scaled bounds (7.4 million states); a 39-cell fault matrix yields the separating models independence requires, and consume-once splits, its consumption clause independent of all six others. A deterministic, LLM-free harness measures them at pinned releases. LangGraph 1.2.9 durably records a second resume value and never consults it, persists schema-invalid state silently, and re-executes durably recorded work after a real SIGKILL: exactly-once across interrupts, at-least-once across crashes, on one API. CrewAI 1.15.2 re-executes completed effect-bearing methods against its written claim; pydantic-graph 1.x cannot resume after a mid-node crash; no two probed frameworks share a conformance profile. Consume-once holds sequentially and fails under concurrent delivery: k processes resuming one parked interrupt fire the gated effect k times, saturation 1.0 in 36 of 40 cells, and the failure crosses hosts. REMIT, a reference sequencer whose Verus-verified recovery core is line-identical to the shipped executable, repairs the fork and validity cells. The cross-process cell is repaired at the read path, and that repair ships: an opt-in gate claims consumption in the shared store, serving one racer and refusing the rest before any node executes.

</details>


### [89] [FedCritic-MIMO: Communication-Efficient Serverless Federated Critic Learning for Massive-MIMO Resource Control in Open and Disaggregated 6G RANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03852)
*Amin Farajzadeh,Melike Erol-Kantarci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FedCritic-MIMO，一种面向开放与分解式 6G RAN 的通信高效无服务器联邦多智能体强化学习框架。该框架中，各小区控制器独立部署、无共享训练器，仅通过干扰感知图对兼容的共享 critic 参数进行点对点交换，实现联合调度、功率分配、波束赋形与干扰管理，在 reuse-1 大规模 MIMO OFDMA 场景中较各基线取得最优性能-通信权衡，critic 通信开销降低 76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放与分解式 6G RAN 中，小区级控制器需独立部署且无法依赖集中式训练或参数服务器聚合。传统集中训练分布式执行（CTDE）方法需要集中式轨迹收集，通信开销大且违背分解式架构的可独立部署特性。因此需要一种无服务器、低通信开销的联邦多智能体强化学习框架，以协调多个独立 RAN 控制器实现 AI 原生资源控制。

Method: FedCritic-MIMO 采用演员-评论家架构：每个基站本地执行独立 actor（不进行 actor 联邦化或集中训练），仅交换共享 critic 参数。关键机制包括：(1) 无线感知事件触发，决定何时进行 critic 通信；(2) 自适应逐层 top-k 稀疏 critic 交换并带误差反馈，压缩通信量；(3) 均衡的干扰感知融合机制，用于聚合收到的 critic 信息。理论层面，在固定策略、冻结目标的 critic 回归模型下，建立了均衡压缩点对点 critic 递归的条件有限时间平稳性与一致性保证。

Result: 在强干扰耦合 reuse-1 仿真中，FedCritic-MIMO 在含启发式、独立学习、集中训练和通信消融等基线中取得最佳性能-通信权衡：在 held-out 吞吐量上最高，改善用户速率分布与平均 SINR，提高 QoS 满意度，并获得最低的单位交付比特干扰成本。相对未压缩的分布式 critic 交换，critic 通信开销降低 76%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无服务器地交换兼容的共享 critic 参数即可有效协调 RAN 控制器，无需集中式轨迹收集或参数服务器聚合，为分解式 6G RAN 的 AI 原生资源控制提供了可行的通信高效方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes FedCritic-MIMO, a communication-efficient serverless federated multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for AI-native resource control across independently deployable cell-level controllers in open and disaggregated 6G RANs. Controllers share no trainer, retain local actors and personalized critic components, and exchange only compatible shared critic parameters. FedCritic-MIMO targets reuse-$1$ multi-cell massive-MIMO OFDMA deployments, where RAN controllers jointly manage user scheduling, per-stream power allocation, beamforming, interference, and long-term QoS with limited inter-controller signaling. Each base station locally executes its actor without centralized training or actor federation, while critic knowledge is exchanged peer-to-peer over an interference-aware graph. It enables this collaboration through wireless-aware event triggering, adaptive layer-wise top-$k$ sparse critic exchange with error feedback, and balanced interference-aware fusion. We establish conditional finite-time stationarity and consensus guarantees for the balanced, compressed peer-to-peer critic recursion under a fixed-policy, frozen-target critic-regression model. In strongly interference-coupled reuse-$1$ simulations, FedCritic-MIMO achieves the best performance-communication tradeoff among heuristic, independent-learning, centralized-training, and communication-ablation baselines. It achieves the highest held-out throughput, improves user-rate distribution and mean SINR, increases QoS satisfaction, and attains the lowest interference cost per delivered bit among learning baselines. It reduces critic-communication overhead by $76\%$ relative to uncompressed distributed critic exchange. These results demonstrate that serverless exchange of compatible shared critic parameters can coordinate RAN controllers without centralized trajectory collection or parameter-server aggregation.

</details>


### [90] [Quantization Effects on Biomedical LLM Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03854)
*Anton Rasmussen,Hong Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现，在使用解码器语言模型作为分类器时，概率提取协议（均值vs求和token对数似然评分）对校准结果的影响是决定性的，其效应超过模型选择本身。研究系统评估了三种Mistral-7B变体在PubMed RCT句分类任务上，不同精度（FP16/INT8/INT4）、提示模板和评分规则对精度与校准的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解码器语言模型用作分类器时，预测类别概率高度依赖实现细节（提示模板、口头标签映射、评分规则），但这些细节很少被视为实验变量。此前研究通常将校准结果归因于模型本身，忽视了协议选择可能主导结论，因此需要系统评估这些因素对模型校准评估的影响。

Method: 在PubMed RCT句子分类任务（n=2000）上对三种Mistral-7B变体（Base、BioMistral、Instruct）进行受控评估，涵盖FP16、INT8、INT4三种精度，使用四种答案文本提示模板，并对比求和与均值token对数似然两种评分规则，同时引入温度缩放和后验校准技术。

Result: 评分规则切换会使模型校准排名反转：BioMistral的期望校准误差从0.097升至0.289，而Instruct从0.237降至0.096，但精度变化不到1个百分点（基础模型变化4-6个百分点）。提示模板选择产生7-24个百分点的精度差异。INT8量化对专门模型影响仅1-2个百分点，但基础模型在某些模板上出现+4.2个百分点变化。温度缩放仅在求和评分规则下有效。

Conclusion: 提示模板设计和评分归一化是评估解码器语言模型校准时的一阶实验决策，其影响甚至超过模型本身差异。研究结果挑战了仅基于单一实现配置评估模型校准的现有实践。

Abstract: When decoder language models are used as classifiers, predicted class probabilities depend on implementation choices, including the prompt template, verbalizer (label-to-token mapping), and scoring rule, that are rarely treated as experimental variables. We present a controlled evaluation of three Mistral-7B variants (Base, BioMistral, and Instruct) on PubMed RCT sentence classification (n=2000) under FP16, INT8, and INT4 precision using four answer-text prompt templates. Our primary finding is that the probability extraction protocol dominates apparent calibration. Switching from summed to mean token log-likelihood scoring reverses the calibration ranking between models: BioMistral average expected calibration error increases from 0.097 to 0.289, whereas Instruct decreases from 0.237 to 0.096, while accuracy changes by less than 1 percentage point for the specialized models but 4-6 percentage points for the base model. Prompt template choice produces accuracy differences of 7-24 percentage points, comparable to or larger than model-level effects. On one template, BioMistral outperforms Instruct although the overall mean favors Instruct by only 1.3 percentage points. For BioMistral and Instruct, INT8 quantization changes accuracy and F1 by only 1-2 percentage points relative to FP16, whereas the base model shows larger INT8 effects on some templates (up to +4.2 percentage points). INT4 produces heterogeneous but non-catastrophic effects. Temperature scaling reduces expected calibration error under summed scoring for both models but only for that scoring rule. A fine-tuned PubMedBERT reference achieves 82.7% accuracy but uses about 176000 labeled training examples, precluding direct comparison. These results demonstrate that prompt template design and scoring normalization are first-order experimental decisions when evaluating decoder language model calibration.

</details>


### [91] [Bi-semantic Chemical Embedder for Joint Representation Learning of SMILES and Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03855)
*David Ming Segura,Jeremy Goumaz,Joshua W. Sin,Bojana Ranković,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CheMatE，一种基于ModernBERT的化学嵌入模型，通过两阶段训练（继续掩码语言建模+Matryoshka对比学习）在统一表示空间中联合捕获分子结构与化学领域自然语言语义，在分子性质预测和科学语言理解任务上取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的领域自适应预训练方法（如在SMILES上进行预训练）常常导致模型过度拟合化学语法，灾难性地遗忘其基础语义能力。需要一种能够同时捕获分子结构和领域特定自然语言语义的化学嵌入模型，避免这种能力退化问题。

Method: CheMatE采用ModernBERT作为骨干架构，通过两阶段训练流程实现双语义表示学习：1）在第一阶段，利用从FineWeb和ChemPile构建的大规模SMILES标注长上下文科学文档语料（分别包含10.4B和11.5B token）进行继续掩码语言建模；2）在第二阶段，通过Multiple Negative Ranking Loss (MNRL)进行Matryoshka对比学习，使用从原始训练语料算法派生的SMILES-文本对合成数据集进行训练。

Result: 在分子性质预测和科学语言理解等下游任务上的评估显示，CheMatE通过自定义数据集与序列训练策略的结合，产生了稳健且高迁移性的表示。在统一结构和上下文信号于单一文本框架方面，CheMatE在专业化学模型和通用语言模型基线上均取得了竞争性性能。

Conclusion: CheMatE验证了将定制语料库与两阶段顺序训练策略相结合的有效性，成功实现了分子结构与自然语言语义的统一表示，为化学领域的多模态嵌入学习提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Transformer models have revolutionized natural language processing (NLP), and text-based molecular representations like SMILES have successfully extended these architectures to chemistry. However, domain-adaptive pre-training often causes models to overfit to chemical syntax, catastrophically forgetting their foundational semantic capabilities. To address this challenge, we introduce CheMatE, a chemistry-oriented embedding model that jointly captures molecular structure and domain-specific natural language within the same representation space. Built on a ModernBERT backbone, CheMatE learns bi-semantic representations through a two-stage training procedure: continued masked language modeling (MLM) followed by a Matryoshka contrastive learning stage via Multiple Negative Ranking Loss (MNRL). First, we train the model using MLM on a novel, large-scale corpus of SMILES-annotated, long-context scientific documents that were constructed and curated from FineWeb and ChemPile (comprising 10.4B and 11.5B tokens, respectively). Subsequently, the model undergoes contrastive learning using a synthetic dataset of SMILES-text pairs algorithmically derived from our original training corpus. This design exposes the model to SMILES-enriched scientific literature, enabling bi-semantic understanding. We evaluate CheMatE across a range of downstream tasks covering molecular property prediction and scientific language understanding. Our results demonstrate that coupling our custom-curated datasets with this sequential training strategy yields robust, highly transferable representations. By effectively unifying structural and contextual signals within a single text-based framework, CheMatE achieves competitive performance across both specialized chemistry models and general-purpose language model baselines.

</details>


### [92] [CRS-Triage: Confidence- and Reliability-Aware Selective Triage under Incomplete Clinical Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03862)
*Guan Qiang,Yushen Chen,Tianlong Liu,David Rotenberg,Ethan H. Kim,Fang Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRS-Triage框架，用于急诊分诊中预测患者病情严重程度等级，通过置信度评分实现选择性决策，并在数据不完整、不可靠或不一致时保持可靠性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊分诊需要在短时间内做出可靠决策，但电子健康记录数据（包括结构化数据和临床文本）常常不完整、不可靠且不一致，现有ML模型依赖完整可靠的数据进行准确预测，难以应对这一挑战。

Method: CRS-Triage框架：1）分别评估结构化数据和临床文本的可靠性；2）联合考虑两种模态间的一致性来估计每个预测的置信度；3）通过置信度与预定义阈值比较，选择性决定模型自主决策或延迟该病例；4）通过惩罚under-triage误差，倾向于将患者分配到略高的病情等级（over-triage），以降低漏检高风险患者的风险。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV-ED数据集上的实验表明，CRS-Triage实现了较强的预测性能，提供了更好的风险-覆盖率权衡，并且在EHR数据不完整、退化或跨模态不一致的情况下保持可靠。

Conclusion: CRS-Triage通过置信度感知的异构数据可靠性评估和选择性决策机制，有效解决了数据质量不佳场景下的急诊分诊预测问题，兼具预测准确性与安全可靠性。

Abstract: Emergency triage requires reliable decisions within a short time period. However, the available electronic health record (EHR) data, including structured data and clinical text, are often incomplete, unreliable, and inconsistent. This makes machine learning (ML)-based triage prediction more challenging, as existing ML models typically rely on complete and reliable EHR data to accurately predict patients' acuity levels. To address this, we propose confidence- and reliability-aware selective triage (CRS-Triage) to predict patients' acuity levels with a confidence score. By comparing the confidence score with a predefined threshold, CRS-Triage can selectively determine whether the model should make the decision or defer the case. Specifically, CRS-Triage separately evaluates the reliability of structured data and clinical text and then jointly considers the consistency between the two modalities to estimate the confidence of each prediction. Moreover, to reduce the risk of missing high-acuity patients, namely under-triage, CRS-Triage prefers to assign patients slightly higher acuity levels, namely over-triage, by penalizing under-triage errors. Experiments on the MIMIC-IV-ED dataset show that CRS-Triage achieves strong predictive performance. It also provides a better risk-coverage trade-off and remains reliable when the available EHR data are incomplete, degraded, or inconsistent across modalities.

</details>


### [93] [GENESIS: Towards Explainable Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03868)
*Abhinav Thorat,Ravi Kumar Kolla,Vishak K Bhat,Harsh Vardhan Singh Chauhan,Niranjan Pedanekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GENESIS是一个可解释的混合因果发现框架，通过将图构建分解为可审计的决策点（三节点结构基序评分、统计证据与领域知识融合），实现100%的决策可追溯性，同时在结构汉明距离上优于纯统计方法并与LLM辅助方法性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法存在两个根本挑战：(1) 纯统计方法在低样本量下缺乏解决结构歧义的能力；(2) LLM辅助混合方法虽能通过语义推理改善结构恢复，但其推理对单条边决策的影响不透明。在无真实DAG的实际应用中，每条边的加入或排除都需要独立且可审计的论证，现有混合方法无法满足这一"决策可追溯性"要求。

Method: GENESIS将图构建分解为可解释的决策点：(1) 首先识别并评分三节点结构基序（链、分叉、对撞子），建立透明的结构先验；(2) 逐步将先验与观测证据整合，仅当统计证据不足时才调用领域知识；(3) 每条边的决策都通过可审计的证据来源（统计证据、马尔可夫毯一致性或显式领域推理）解决。

Result: 实验表明GENESIS在所有设置下实现100%的决策可追溯性，且在多数基准数据集上、所有样本量范围内，以结构汉明距离(SHD)为指标，一致优于纯统计CD方法，同时达到与最先进的LLM辅助方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: GENESIS证明了"可解释性"可以成为因果发现中的一等公民目标，在满足决策可追溯性的额外约束下，不仅没有牺牲性能，反而在多数场景下超越了纯统计方法，实现了可解释性与性能的双赢。

Abstract: Causal Discovery (CD) from observational data faces two fundamental challenges. First, purely statistical methods often lack the power to resolve structural ambiguities in low-sample regimes. Second, although LLM-assisted hybrid approaches improve structure recovery through semantic reasoning, the influence of that reasoning on individual edge decisions remains largely opaque. Consequently, existing hybrid methods fail to satisfy a fundamental requirement: explaining why a particular edge is included or excluded in the learned directed acyclic graph (DAG). This is critical in real-world applications, where no ground-truth DAG exists and every structural decision must be independently justified. We formalize this requirement as decision traceability, requiring every inferred edge to be supported by auditable statistical evidence, Markov Blanket consistency, or explicit domain reasoning. We propose GENESIS, an explainable hybrid CD framework that decomposes graph construction into interpretable decision points. GENESIS first identifies and scores three-node structural motifs, including chains, forks, and colliders, to establish transparent structural priors, then progressively refines the graph by integrating these priors with observational evidence, invoking domain knowledge only when statistical evidence is insufficient. By design, every edge decision is resolved through an auditable source of evidence. Experiments show that GENESIS achieves 100% decision traceability across all settings, establishing explainability as a first-class objective in causal discovery. Despite this additional requirement, GENESIS consistently outperforms purely statistical CD methods on the majority of benchmark datasets across all sample regimes in terms of Structural Hamming Distance (SHD), while achieving performance comparable to state-of-the-art LLM-assisted approaches.

</details>


### [94] [Enhancing VLM Reward Models Through Structure-Aware Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03875)
*Pyrros Koussios,Chenhao Li,Xin Chen,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构感知微调（SAFT），一种无需人工标注的自监督方法，通过LoRA适配器利用任务内在结构先验来规整VLM奖励模型的潜在空间，从而在线去噪不完美的奖励信号，加速策略收敛并提升对齐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于VLM的文本-观测相似度奖励虽然避免了手动设计奖励，但往往噪声大、不可靠，难以直接用于部署。现有方法缺乏在没有真值监督下修正这些奖励信号的有效手段。

Method: SAFT是一种简单自监督方法，在线精炼VLM产生的有噪声奖励信号。它利用任务固有的结构先验，通过LoRA适配器对VLM潜在空间进行正则化，从而无需地面真值监督即可去噪。

Result: SAFT在不同能力等级的基础模型上均能一致地去噪奖励景观，带来更快的策略收敛和显著更好的对齐（以EPIC距离衡量），优于底层基座模型的表现。

Conclusion: RL失败往往源于结构脆弱性而非语义理解错误；用任务结构归纳偏置替代大量人工偏好标注，为稳定文本条件RL提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Designing effective reward functions remains a major bottleneck in Reinforcement Learning (RL). Recent work uses large foundation Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as reward models, computing text-observation similarity to bypass manual reward engineering. Although promising, these rewards are often noisy and unreliable, limiting their direct utility during deployment. We present Structure-Aware Fine-Tuning (SAFT), a simple, self-supervised method that refines these imperfect reward signals online without access to ground-truth supervision. SAFT leverages intrinsic structural priors to regularize the VLM's latent space via LoRA adapters. We rigorously evaluate SAFT across a spectrum of base model capabilities to demonstrate its versatility. Our results show that SAFT consistently denoises the reward landscape, yielding faster policy convergence and substantially improved alignment (EPIC distance) relative to the underlying base model, suggesting that failures can often be attributed to structural brittleness rather than semantic misunderstanding. By replacing extensive human preference annotation with structural inductive biases inherent to the task, SAFT offers a scalable path for stabilizing text-conditioned RL and underscores the broader value of incorporating task structure as a general inductive bias.

</details>


### [95] [Operationally Feasible Synthetic Power-Grid Scenarios via Learning the AC-Operable Joint Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03878)
*Chenhan Xiao,Xinyu He,Haoran Li,Hanghang Tong,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可行性感知的分布学习框架，通过分层扩散模型直接学习AC可运行的电网联合分布（拓扑、支路参数、负荷曲线），在生成阶段即嵌入AC潮流收敛与运行约束，替代事后验证/优化，从而提高合成电网场景的AC可行性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成电网生成方法通过事后验证、优化或物理感知生成来提升结构真实性和运行可行性，但生成的场景仍可能存在低AC可行性与鲁棒性问题，限制了其在下游电力系统研究中的实际应用价值。

Method: 提出一个可行性感知的分层扩散分布学习框架。将AC潮流收敛与运行约束嵌入基于扩散模型的分布学习中，使生成器本身就能产出运行可行的电网场景。层级架构将高维生成任务分解为三个阶段：拓扑与母线属性生成、基于已生成结构的支路参数生成、基于网络结构与电气特性的负荷曲线生成。

Result: 在基准系统上的实验表明，该框架显著提升了运行可行性和紧急情况鲁棒性，同时保持较强的统计保真度，并消除了基于优化的后处理步骤。

Conclusion: 将AC可行性约束嵌入生成过程（而非事后处理）的扩散模型能够同时兼顾统计保真度与运行可行性，为合成电网场景生成提供了一种无需优化后处理的可行路径。

Abstract: Synthetic power-grid scenarios are essential for planning, resilience assessment, contingency analysis, and data-driven power-system applications. Recent synthetic grid generation methods have improved structural realism and operational feasibility by incorporating engineering knowledge through post-generation validation, optimization, or physics-aware generation. However, generated scenarios may still exhibit low AC feasibility and robustness, limiting their practical value for downstream power-system studies. This paper proposes a feasibility-aware distribution-learning framework that learns the AC-operable joint distribution of network topology, branch electrical parameters, and time-varying load profiles. Instead of enforcing feasibility after generation, the proposed framework incorporates AC power-flow convergence and operational constraints into hierarchical diffusion-based distribution learning. This enables the generator itself to produce operationally feasible grid scenarios through efficient diffusion sampling. The hierarchical architecture decomposes the high-dimensional generation task into three engineering-motivated stages: topology and bus-attribute generation, branch-parameter generation conditioned on the generated structure, and load-profile generation conditioned on both network structure and electrical characteristics. Experiments on benchmark systems demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly improves operational feasibility and contingency robustness while maintaining strong statistical fidelity and eliminating optimization-based post-processing.

</details>


### [96] [Cross-Model KV Cache Transfer in LLM Families: A Closed-Form Linear Mapping for Prefill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03893)
*Taekyung Heo,Rasoul Shafipour,Ritchie Zhao,Maximilian Golub,Mohammad Mahdi Kamani,Ritika Borkar,Makesh Tarun Chandran,Pantea Zardoshti,Bita Darvish Rouhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出跨模型KV缓存迁移方法，通过线性映射器将源模型的KV缓存直接映射到目标模型，跳过预填充（prefill）过程。作者发现跨模型KV对之间存在显著的线性结构，并设计了三步走的岭回归映射器（去RoPE、选层拼接、岭拟合），在多个模型家族上保留73-98%的独立预填充准确率，且推理速度比重新预填充快2.7-25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中经常在不同大小的模型之间切换（如成本-质量级联、对话中途切换、路由等），每次切换都迫使接收模型从头执行预填充，带来大量计算开销。若能将源模型的KV缓存直接迁移给目标模型，可显著省去预填充成本。

Method: 方法包含三个核心步骤：（1）对每个目标层选择top-k个最具预测性的源层并拼接其KV作为输入；（2）在映射前去除键中的RoPE（旋转位置编码），使映射与位置无关，可跨上下文长度复用；（3）在小规模校准集（500条FineWeb-Edu序列，每条1024个token）上拟合每头独立的岭回归。此外，在岭回归失效的模型对上使用非线性MLP进行补救。

Result: 在三个模型家族的六组模型对上，线性映射器在四对模型上保留了73-98%的独立预填充准确率，但有两对模型退化严重。非线性MLP在失败案例上恢复了最多+37个百分点（HellaSwag）的保留率。映射器运行速度比重新预填充快2.7-25倍，且在多次对话切换中保持稳定。

Conclusion: 跨模型KV缓存迁移是可行的且实用的，线性岭回归映射器在大多数模型对下表现良好，非线性MLP可弥补少数失效场景。该方法能显著加速模型切换场景下的推理，为生产环境中的模型级联和路由提供了新范式。

Abstract: Production deployments often swap between different-sized models in a family for cost-quality cascading, mid-conversation switching, and routing, and each swap forces the receiver to repay the prefill from scratch. We propose cross-model KV cache transfer, where the receiver reuses the source's KV cache, skipping prefill. We find that cross-model KV has substantial linear structure across matched-KV pairs, where source and target share KV head count and per-head dimension. On Qwen3 14B->32B, one source layer explains 56% of variance in the target's keys and 32% in values, rising to 79% and 65% with multiple source layers. Building on this, we design a closed-form ridge mapper that operates per head and proceeds in three steps. First, for each target layer we select the top-k most predictive source layers and concatenate their KV as input. Second, we strip RoPE from the keys before mapping, so the fit is position-free and reusable across context lengths. Third, we fit ridge regression on a small calibration set of 500 FineWeb-Edu sequences of 1,024 tokens each. Surprisingly, across six pairs in three families, this linear mapper retains 73-98% of the receiver's standalone-prefill accuracy on four pairs, while two degrade sharply. A nonlinear MLP recovers up to +37 pp HellaSwag retention on the failures. The mapper runs 2.7-25x faster than re-prefill and remains stable across multi-turn handoff, making cross-model KV cache transfer practical.

</details>


### [97] [Sparse Weight Decomposition for Efficient Circuit Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03913)
*Chuanhao Yan,Xuhan Huang,Yawen Duan,Zhenfei Yin,Hang Zhao,Bryan Dai,Jie Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sparse Weight Decomposition (SWD)方法，通过将预训练线性投影的权重矩阵分解为两个稀疏因子，其共享中间坐标作为可寻址的电路单元，无需训练独立的替换网络即可支持电路提取工作流。SWD在保持与强基线相当的精度的同时，仅需不到1%的数据，并在GPT-2、Qwen2.5和Qwen3.5-27B等模型上展现了更好的电路充分性和必要性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密预训练Transformer不自然地暴露可解释单元，现有方法通过学习辅助稀疏表示或训练稀疏模型来获得这些单元，但会带来大量额外计算，同时可能在分析表示与原始预训练模型之间引入保真度差距。需要一个无需大量额外训练即可获得可解释电路单元的方案。

Method: SWD将每个预训练的权重矩阵重新参数化为两个稀疏因子的乘积形式，两个因子的共享中间坐标作为独立可寻址的电路单元。该方法支持与学习稀疏特征方法相同的评分、选择和消融电路提取工作流，无需训练单独的替换网络。SWD还提供了零数据的变体，适用于逐步骤分析等更广泛的机制可解释性分析。

Result: 在单矩阵替换中，SWD的保真度与Transcoder等强基线相当，但使用的训练数据不到基线的1%。在匹配的替换保真度下，SWD在GPT-2、Qwen2.5和Qwen3.5-27B上以更少的活跃读写边和选中的单元达到相同的电路充分性和必要性目标。SWD在全模型替换（所有注意力和MLP权重矩阵）中，通过对非零因子值进行微调后仍然有效。

Conclusion: SWD是一种高效且有零数据变体的电路提取方法，在保持预训练模型保真度的同时大幅降低了额外计算和数据需求，并能在更大规模模型上有效工作，为机制可解释性分析提供了更实用的工具。

Abstract: Dense pretrained transformers do not naturally expose interpretable units for circuit extraction. Existing approaches obtain such units by learning auxiliary sparse representations or training sparse models, incurring substantial additional computation while potentially introducing a fidelity gap between the representation being analyzed and the original pretrained model. We propose Sparse Weight Decomposition (SWD), which reparameterizes pretrained linear projections by factorizing each weight matrix into two sparse factors whose shared intermediate coordinates serve as individually addressable circuit units. Without training a separate replacement network, this parametric representation supports the same scoring, selection, and ablation circuit extraction workflow used for methods that learn sparse features. Across single-matrix replacements, SWD matches the held-out fidelity achieved by Transcoder and other strong baselines while using less than 1% of the data that those baselines use to train their replacements. For matched replacement fidelity, SWD reaches the same circuit sufficiency and necessity targets with fewer active read/write edges and selected units across tasks on GPT-2, Qwen2.5, and Qwen3.5-27B. We further show that SWD remains effective for full-model replacement of all attention and MLP weight matrices after fine-tuning the nonzero factor values. Finally, SWD also features a zero-data variant, allowing broader use of mechanistic interpretability analysis (e.g., per-step analysis).

</details>


### [98] [Trajectory inference via Acceleration Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03916)
*Bartolo Dazzini,Giovanni Conforti,Alain Durmus,Aram-Alexandre Pooladian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Acceleration Matching (AM)的新算法用于轨迹推断（trajectory inference）问题。该算法将插值问题提升到相空间，并通过回归一个显式的条件加速度场来生成平滑随机轨迹，避免了现有方法中昂贵的预处理或模拟式训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹推断是许多科学领域的基础问题：给定离散时间点上未配对的观测快照，目标是生成与数据最相似且能插值的平滑轨迹。现有算法存在计算挑战：要么依赖预处理子程序来强制平滑性，要么依赖基于模拟的训练目标，两者都可能很昂贵。

Method: AM算法的核心是将原始插值问题提升到相空间（phase space），然后回归到一个显式的条件加速度场（conditional acceleration field），该场能诱导出随机的、平滑的、与给定边缘分布一致的轨迹。训练仅需位置数据，避免轨迹模拟，且无需昂贵的预处理。

Result: 大量数值实验表明，AM在多个已有文献中的基准问题上与现有算法具有竞争力或表现更优。

Conclusion: AM算法通过相空间提升和条件加速度场回归，有效克服了现有轨迹推断方法的计算瓶颈，在保持精度的同时显著降低训练成本。

Abstract: Trajectory inference is a fundamental problem in many scientific domains: given a collection of unpaired snapshots of observations at discrete time points, the goal is to generate smooth trajectories that best resemble and interpolate the data. Existing algorithms exhibit computational challenges: they either rely on preprocessing subroutines to enforce smoothness or on simulation-based training objectives, both of which can be expensive. In order to overcome these limitations, we propose a new algorithm called Acceleration Matching (\texttt{AM}). Our approach consists of lifting the original interpolation problem to phase space and then regressing onto an explicit conditional acceleration field that induces random, smooth trajectories that agree with the prescribed marginals. Importantly, our resulting training algorithm only requires positional data, avoids trajectory simulation during training, and is devoid of expensive preprocessing. We provide ample numerical evidence suggesting that \texttt{AM} is competitive with or superior to existing algorithms on several benchmark problems from the existing literature.

</details>


### [99] [PRISM: Powerful Time Series to Image (TS2I) Representations for Multivariate Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03926)
*Mateusz Smendowski,Kamil Faber,Piotr Nawrocki,Nathalie Japkowicz,Roberto Corizzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM，一个即插即用的元工作流，用于系统性地构建和评估基于图像表示的多变量时间序列异常检测（TSAD）。通过超过7,000个实验，证明精心设计的PRISM配置可媲美24个时域基线方法，在14个数据集的10个上取得最佳VUS-PR，平均提升41%。同时识别出"通道化"（channelization）是关键的未充分研究的构建维度，并提出新的MSM统计方案，比PCA方案提升11-27%。此外，ImageNet预训练编码器可有效迁移至TSAD，冻结编码器保留微调性能的92%且训练快1.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列异常检测对表示选择高度敏感，尽管将时间序列转换为图像在预测和分类任务中已被证明有效，但在TSAD中，如何将多变量高维序列映射到多通道图像、以及视觉骨干网络能否匹配时域基线性能仍不清楚。现有工作缺乏系统性的探索和评估框架。

Method: PRISM作为一个即插即用的元工作流，系统化地构建和评估基于图像表示的多变量TSAD。研究中识別并深入分析了"通道化"这一关键设计维度，即多通道图像通道维度的构建方式；并提出了MSM（一种基于统计的方案）作为更优的通道构建策略。同时评估了ImageNet预训练编码器（冻结与微调两种模式）在TSAD中的迁移效果。

Result: 基于超过7,000个实验，精心设计的PRISM配置在14个数据集中的10个上超越24个时域基线方法，取得最佳VUS-PR，平均提升41%；MSM方案相较PCA方案在通道化方面取得11-27%的增益；冻结的ImageNet预训练编码器保留微调性能的92%，同时训练速度快1.8倍。

Conclusion: 基于图像的表示在TSAD中是可行且有竞争力的方案，设计得当的PRISM配置可匹敌或超越时域基线；通道化是影响性能的关键设计维度；预训练视觉编码器可以高效迁移到TSAD任务。这为多变量TSAD提供了新的研究方向和实用工具。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) underpins applications in predictive maintenance, finance, and cloud computing, however performance remains sensitive to representation choices, especially in multivariate settings. While transforming time series into images has shown success in forecasting and classification, it remains unclear how multivariate, high-dimensional series should be mapped to multi-channel images and whether vision backbones can match time-domain baselines in TSAD. We introduce PRISM, a plug-and-play meta-workflow enabling systematic construction and evaluation of image-based representations for multivariate TSAD. Our evaluation spanning over 7,000 experiments shows that well-designed PRISM configurations are competitive with 24 time-domain baselines, achieving the best VUS-PR on 10 of 14 datasets, with an average improvement of 41% over the best competing method on those datasets. Further, we identify channelization - how the channel dimension of multi-channel images is constructed - as a critical and previously understudied design dimension, and introduce MSM, a novel statistics-based scheme achieving 11-27% gains over PCA-based alternatives. Finally, ImageNet-pretrained encoders transfer effectively to TSAD, with frozen encoders retaining 92% of fine-tuned performance while training 1.8 times faster. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Smendowski/PRISM.

</details>


### [100] [A Physics-Flavored Transformer Network for Parametrizing Contraction Dynamics of Engineered Skeletal Muscle Tissues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03927)
*Mattias Luber,Timo Betz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理风味神经网络（PFNN），将拉伸指数物理模型集成到CNN-Transformer架构中，用于自动提取工程化骨骼肌组织（ESMs）力-时间曲线中的动力学参数，实现高通量的动力学表型分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程化骨骼肌组织的功能表征通常依赖简单的峰值力指标，忽略了关键的动力学信息。虽然力学模型可以捕捉这些动态，但其数学复杂性阻碍了规模化应用和广泛普及。

Method: PFNN采用CNN-Transformer架构，将拉伸指数（stretched-exponential）物理模型嵌入其中，直接从力-时间曲线中提取具有物理意义的参数。为解决标注生物数据稀缺的问题，采用混合训练范式：先在合成数据上建立"物理直觉"，再在未标注的真实测量数据上进行无监督自对齐。

Result: 该物理风味方法在多种收缩表型和细胞系（包括杜氏肌营养不良模型）上实现了高保真度参数化，展示了可扩展且自改进的流水线能力，弥合了理想生物物理与噪声体外数据之间的差距。
