Conclusion: PFNN为高通量生物物理研究提供了稳健工具，克服了传统力学模型复杂度高、难以规模化的瓶颈，推动ESMs动力学表征的自动化与普及。

Abstract: Engineered Skeletal Muscle Tissues (ESMs) have become a key structure for biomedical disease modeling and pharmacological screening, yet their functional characterization often relies on simplistic metrics like peak force, discarding critical kinetic information. This is partially due to the high level of mathematical complexity which mechanistic models introduce to capture these dynamics. Hence, exactly the complexity prevents scalable application and widespread adaptation in the field. Here we present a Physics-Flavored Neural Network (PFNN) that automates the kinetic phenotyping of ESMs. Our architecture integrates a stretched-exponential physical model into a CNN-Transformer, enabling the extraction of physically meaningful parameters directly from force-time profiles. To address the scarcity of labeled biological data, we employ a hybrid training paradigm: the model develops a "physical intuition" on synthetic data before undergoing unsupervised self-alignment on unlabeled real-world measurements. Our results demonstrate that this physics-flavored approach achieves high-fidelity parameterization across diverse contractile phenotypes and cell lines, including Duchenne Muscular Dystrophy models. Our scalable, self-improving pipeline bridges the gap between idealized biophysics and noisy \emph{in vitro} data, providing a robust tool for high-throughput biophysical research.

</details>


### [101] [Latent Reward Registers for Diffusion Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03929)
*Yuanshen Guan,Zipeng Feng,Zhiwei Xiong,Peiqin Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Reward Registers机制，通过在冻结的Diffusion Transformer（DiT）输入序列中前置可学习、位置无关的寄存器token，直接从中间噪声潜在表示中估计终端偏好奖励。该机制产生密集可微的奖励信号，并衍生出两种对齐策略：训练阶段的RG-OPD（奖励梯度在线策略蒸馏）和推理阶段的RGS（奖励引导采样），分别在训练效率和推理性能上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型与人类偏好对齐方法通常依赖稀疏的终端奖励（仅在最终生成样本上评估），这导致多步去噪过程中存在严重的时间信用分配问题（temporal credit-assignment），即难以确定中间哪一步对最终偏好的贡献最大。

Method: 提出Latent Reward Registers：（1）在冻结DiT输入序列中前置可学习、位置无关的寄存器token，直接从中间噪声潜变量估计终端偏好，构成独立读出机制，不改变生成器隐藏状态或速度场；（2）训练策略RG-OPD沿在线策略轨迹蒸馏奖励引导更新，避免标准策略梯度的高计算成本rollout；（3）推理策略RGS通过幅度匹配的奖励梯度引导采样轨迹，无需参数更新。

Result: （1）高噪声水平（u=0.8）下，寄存器在评估的潜在奖励模型中达到最高成对准确率；（2）RG-OPD超越在线强化学习基线，同时GPU耗时最多减少33倍；（3）RGS在无训练方法中达到新SOTA，同时提升对齐指标和感知质量指标。

Conclusion: 通过奖励寄存器实现的密集奖励信号有效解决了稀疏奖励导致的信用分配困境，且训练和推理两种对齐策略均验证了该机制的有效性与高效性，代码和权重已开源。

Abstract: Aligning diffusion models with human preferences usually relies on a sparse terminal reward evaluated on the final generated samples, presenting a severe temporal credit-assignment challenge across the multi-step denoising process. We propose Latent Reward Registers, a mechanism that estimates terminal preference directly from intermediate noisy latents by prepending learnable, position-free register tokens to the input sequence of a frozen Diffusion Transformer (DiT). This independent readout mechanism extracts latent reward evidence without altering the generator's hidden states or velocity field. The resulting dense, differentiable reward signal throughout the full denoising process facilitates two alignment strategies. For training, Reward-Gradient On-Policy Distillation (RG-OPD) distills reward-guided updates along on-policy trajectories, bypassing the computationally expensive rollouts of standard policy gradients. For inference, Reward-Guided Sampling (RGS) steers trajectories via magnitude-matched reward gradients without parameter updates. Empirically, at high noise levels (u = 0.8), the registers reach the highest pairwise accuracy among the evaluated latent reward models. Furthermore, RG-OPD outperforms online reinforcement learning baselines while reducing GPU hours by up to 33x, and RGS establishes a new state-of-the-art among training-free methods, strictly enhancing both alignment and perceptual metrics. Code and weights are available at https://github.com/Guanys-dar/latent-reward-register

</details>


### [102] [Muon Meets Mamba: Spectral Optimization for State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03941)
*Arslan Battalov,Karim Kramin,Alexander Markotenko,Sofia Sinitsina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Mamba-2 130M模型上对比了Muon优化器与AdamW，发现Muon的收益具有局部性：仅在输出投影层使用Muon即可优于在输入投影层或两者同时使用，且优势主要体现在token效率上，不受语料和训练预算影响，条件数优化并非收益的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在Transformer模型上表现优异，但其在状态空间模型（如Mamba）上的行为缺乏系统研究，作者希望在SSM架构下填补这一空白，并探究Muon收益的来源。

Method: 在可控协议下对Mamba-2 130M进行对比实验，仅改变使用Muon训练的权重组（输入投影、输出投影、或两者），并与AdamW基线比较；在两种语料和两个token预算下评估，并扩展训练至超过计算最优点；同时测量各投影层的条件数以检验其假设。

Result: Muon仅在输出投影层使用时效果最佳，优于仅在输入投影层或两者都使用的情况；收益主要体现在token效率上，在多种语料与token预算下均成立，且持续训练时优势仍然保持；Muon确实降低了所训练投影层的条件数，但条件数更好的输入投影层并未带来收益，说明条件数不能解释该增益。

Conclusion: Muon在Mamba-2上的收益是局部且非直观的，集中在输出投影层，且与条件数改善无关；这提示Muon的加速机制可能比简单的谱范数几何更复杂，需要进一步研究其有效性的底层原理。

Abstract: Muon is a recent optimizer that orthogonalizes the update to each weight matrix with a Newton-Schulz iteration, which performs steepest descent under the spectral norm. Almost all the evidence for it comes from Transformer models, and its behavior on state-space models is largely unreported. We compare Muon with AdamW on Mamba-2 130M under a controlled protocol that varies only which weight groups are trained with Muon. The benefit is localized. Muon on the output projection alone beats Muon on the input projection or on both. The advantage is mainly one of token efficiency. It holds on two corpora and two token budgets, and persists when training continues well past the compute-optimal point. Conditioning does not explain the gain. Muon lowers the condition number of whichever projection it trains, but the better-conditioned input projection is not the one that helps.

</details>


### [103] [Assessment of Conditional Diffusion Model for Synthetic Histopathology Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03990)
*Seyed Kahaki,Shijie Li,Weijie Chen,Nicholas Petrick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对合成组织病理学图像质量评估方法进行系统研究，指出现有评估指标（FID和IS）因依赖ImageNet预训练特征提取器而在病理图像上存在局限，提出基于病理学预训练基础模型的改进FID和IS指标，并辅以precision-recall类指标进行补充评估。通过条件去噪扩散模型在四个基准数据集上生成不同质量特征的合成数据，验证了病理特异性指标与下游核分割任务（AJI+和Dice）的相关性，发现改进的Inception Score与下游分割性能高度相关（r=0.6096），并揭示训练数据多样性比单图视觉保真度对分割模型性能提升更为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理学领域面临数据稀缺问题，合成组织病理图像生成被视为潜在解决方案，但现有评估方法可能无法充分反映合成数据在医疗应用中的实际质量。传统指标如FID和IS依赖于ImageNet预训练特征提取器，与病理图像领域差异较大，可能导致评估失真，亟需开发更适用于病理学场景的合成数据质量评估方法。

Method: 首先指出现有FID和IS指标因ImageNet预训练特征提取器而在病理图像上存在局限，继而提出使用数字病理数据集上预训练的基础模型构建修改版FID和IS指标，并补充precision-recall类指标作为额外质量评估；采用条件去噪扩散模型和两步训练方法，在四个基准数据集上生成具有系统性质量差异的合成数据集；最后将合成数据质量指标与下游核分割性能（AJI+和Dice系数）进行相关性分析。

Result: 病理特异性指标展现出更强的判别力：改进的Inception Score与下游AJI+的相关性达r=0.6096（p=0.0122），而原始IS的相关性仅为r=0.0708（p=0.7944）。此外，研究发现合成训练数据的多样性增加与分割模型性能的正相关性高于单纯提升单幅生成图像的视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 病理学特定领域的评估指标比通用图像生成指标更具判别力，能够更准确地反映合成组织病理图像在下游任务中的实际价值；合成数据的多样性对下游模型性能的提升作用比单图视觉质量更重要，这对未来合成病理数据的生成策略和评估规范具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Synthetic histopathology image generation has emerged as an approach that may address data scarcity in computational pathology, yet current evaluation methodologies may not fully assess synthetic data quality for medical applications. This work investigates and addresses limitations in existing evaluation metrics, investigating an approach for assessing synthetic histopathology image quality through domain-specific metrics and downstream task validation. We show that conventional synthetic data evaluation metrics such as Frechet Inception Distance (FID) and Inception Score (IS) may have limitations when applied to histopathology images due to their reliance on ImageNet-pretrained feature extractors. To address these limitations, we propose for consideration modified FID and IS approaches utilizing foundation models pretrained on digital pathology datasets, supplemented by precision-recall based metrics as part of an additional quality assessment. Using conditional denoising diffusion models trained on four benchmark datasets, with a two-step training approach, we generated synthetic datasets with systematically varied quality characteristics. We also measured the correlation between the synthetic data quality metrics with downstream nuclei segmentation performance using common metrics including the aggregated Jaccard index (AJI+) and the Dice coefficient. The study results suggest that pathology-specific metrics may provide improved discriminative power. Specifically, the modified Inception Score indicates higher correlation with downstream task performance (r=0.6096 with AJI+, p=0.0122), compared to the original IS (r=0.0708, p=0.7944). Our observations indicate that increasing the variety of generated training data has a higher positive correlation with segmentation model performance than improving the visual fidelity of individual generated images.

</details>


### [104] [Test-Time Scaling in Reasoning LLMs: Inference Regimes, Evaluation, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04001)
*Mohsen Hariri,Weicong Chen,Nahal Shahini,Vikash Singh,Kai Ye,Amirhossein Samandar,Debargha Ganguly,Sreehari Sankar,Yanyan Zhang,Shouren Wang,Jerry Peng,Biyao Zhang,Michael Hinczewski,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统地阐述了测试时扩展（test-time scaling）的三个轴：形式化推理结构（单轨迹顺序扩展、叶级扩展与前缀级扩展）、评估原则（区分端到端系统性能与候选库诊断，引入评估配置文件），以及可复现性要求（区分精确重放与分布可复现）。作者还组织了开源权重推理生态系统，并在多个基准上应用这些原则，发布了超过20亿条完整推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前"测试时扩展"一词涵盖了多种结构迥异的推理算法——沿单条轨迹扩展、采样完整候选并通过投票/验证聚合、或在未完成的部分状态上搜索。这些算法在统计结构、计算核算和失败模式上各不相同，但常被当作可互换的单一标量"预算"，或在报告准确率时不说明所用推理协议，导致不同研究之间的结果难以比较。

Method: 本文沿三个轴展开：(1) 将测试时扩展形式化为自回归模型隐式前缀树上的有预算推理，区分三种结构机制：单轨迹顺序扩展、带终端归约的叶级扩展、前缀级扩展；(2) 将评估对象视为整个推理系统，引入评估配置文件，其坐标和简单函数可恢复或界定常见的重复采样指标，并规定协议匹配的计算与不确定性报告；(3) 明确推理协议的可复现性要求，区分精确重放与分布可复现，并识别支持每种复现所需的工件。

Result: 作者组织并建立了开源权重推理生态系统的模型侧与接口机制分类，将上述原则应用于广泛知识、符号推理和竞赛数学等多个基准，并组装了超过20亿条完整推理轨迹用于发布，包含逐步丰富的验证器和token级信号。

Conclusion: 不同测试时扩展算法不应被混为一谈，需要按推理结构、评估配置文件和可复现性三个维度进行系统化区分与标准化报告，以促进研究之间的可比性和可复现性。

Abstract: Large language models can solve substantially harder reasoning problems with more inference-time compute. The term "test-time scaling," however, now covers diverse inference algorithms that extend deliberation along a single trajectory, sample completed candidates and aggregate them through voting or verification, or search over unfinished partial states. These algorithms differ in their statistical structure, compute accounting, and failure modes. Treating these procedures as interchangeable under a single scalar "budget," or reporting accuracy without the inference protocol that produced it, makes results difficult to compare across studies. We develop a systematic account of test-time scaling along three axes. First, we formalize test-time scaling as budgeted inference over the implicit prefix tree of an autoregressive model and distinguish three structural regimes: single-trajectory sequential scaling, leaf-level scaling with terminal reduction, and prefix-level scaling. Second, we treat the evaluated object as the entire inference system and develop evaluation principles that separate end-to-end system performance from candidate-bank diagnostics. We introduce an evaluation profile whose coordinates and simple functionals recover or bound common repeated-sampling metrics, and prescribe protocol-matched reporting of compute and uncertainty. Third, we specify reproducibility requirements for inference protocols, distinguishing exact replay from distributional reproducibility and identifying the artifacts needed to support each. We also organize the open-weight reasoning ecosystem by model-side and interface mechanisms, apply these principles to broad-knowledge, symbolic-reasoning, and competition-mathematics benchmarks, and assemble over 2 billion full reasoning traces for release with progressively richer verifier and token-level signals.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [NeuroMosaic: Anatomically Grounded Multimodal Large Language Modeling for Molecularly Aware Glioma Reasoning from 3D MRI and Clinical Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03187)
*Yantong Liu,Zheyu Zhang,Runpeng Liu,Mu Xitang,Seong-Yoon Shin,Hyun-Ae Lee*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: NeuroMosaic是一个3D多模态医学大语言模型，通过将多序列脑部MRI转换为解剖结构索引的区域性token，并与临床叙事和分子概念对齐，实现了可溯源、有证据支撑的神经肿瘤学诊断推理。在四个胶质瘤队列中展现了优异的内部和外部性能，并通过证据定位准确性和定向删除实验验证了其推理的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态医学大语言模型在神经肿瘤学领域存在结构性弱点：三维体积证据被压缩为通用视觉token而丢失空间信息，且诊断结论缺乏与MRI区域的可审计关联。这使得模型在临床决策中难以提供可验证、可追溯的依据。

Method: NeuroMosaic的架构由四个核心组件构成：(1)多分辨率体积tokenizer，用于将多序列MRI转换为区域token；(2)神经解剖图谱路由器，将token映射到解剖结构索引；(3)分子概念记忆模块，对齐分子生物学信息；(4)选择性风险控制机制。整体实现了从体积影像到解剖索引token、再到临床叙事与分子概念的多层次对齐。

Result: 在四个胶质瘤队列中，NeuroMosaic的内部亚型macro-F1达0.827，外部macro-F1为0.784、0.761和0.742。在UPenn-GBM数据集上，相比最强基线提升3.6个百分点（95% CI: 1.8-5.4，校正p=0.0018），IDH、1p/19q和MGMT的AUROC分别为0.918、0.861和0.781。证据定位准确率0.703，定向删除证据使正确答案概率下降0.187（随机删除仅为0.046）。

Conclusion: 解剖结构索引路由被证明是一种可测量的机制，能够实现准确、有依据、且经过校准的体积医学语言推理，为多模态医学大语言模型在神经肿瘤学中的可信应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Multimodal medical large language models remain structurally weak for neuro-oncology because volumetric evidence is compressed into generic visual tokens and diagnostic conclusions often lack an auditable link to MRI regions. We present NeuroMosaic, a 3D multimodal language model that converts multi-sequence brain MRI into anatomy-indexed regional tokens, aligns them with clinical narrative and molecular concepts, and generates evidence-linked outputs. The architecture combines a multi-resolution volumetric tokenizer, a neuroanatomical graph router, a molecular concept memory, and selective risk control. Across four glioma cohorts, NeuroMosaic achieved an internal subtype macro-F1 of 0.827 and external macro-F1 values of 0.784, 0.761, and 0.742. On UPenn-GBM, it improved over the strongest matched-input baseline by 3.6 percentage points (95% CI: 1.8 to 5.4, adjusted p = 0.0018), with IDH, 1p/19q, and MGMT AUROCs of 0.918, 0.861, and 0.781. Evidence pointing accuracy reached 0.703, and targeted evidence deletion reduced correct-answer probability by 0.187, compared with 0.046 for random deletion. These results establish anatomy-indexed routing as a measurable mechanism for accurate, grounded, and calibrated volumetric medical-language reasoning.

</details>


### [106] [Impacts of Single-objective Landscapes on Multi-objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03266)
*Shoichiro Tanaka,Keiki Takadama,Hiroyuki Sato*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了多目标优化问题与其中包含的多个单目标优化问题之间的关系，通过局部最优网络和Pareto最优网络的图结构分析，发现大多数Pareto最优解可从单目标局部最优解到达，且该趋势随目标数和目标相关性增加而增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多目标优化问题与其子单目标优化问题之间的结构关系，验证是否可以通过求解单目标问题来辅助多目标优化。

Method: 针对组合优化问题，将多目标Pareto最优网络与各单目标局部最优网络分别构建为图结构，将整体网络划分为子图（组件），通过重叠关系刻画单目标局部最优网络与多目标Pareto最优网络之间的关联，并在多目标景观问题上进行实验分析。

Result: 大多数Pareto最优解可从单目标局部最优解到达；该趋势随目标数量和目标相关性的增加而更加明显；协变量数量影响两类网络之间的交叉关联数。

Conclusion: 搜索单目标问题可作为多目标优化的有效线索，为利用单目标优化方法辅助多目标优化提供了理论支持。

Abstract: This work revealed a relationship between a multi-objective optimization problem and single-objective optimization problems that exist in the multi-objective problem. This work focused on combinatorial problems and investigated the relations between the local optima networks of the single-objective problems and the Pareto optima network of the multi-objective problem. Each of their networks has a graph structure. We divided the entire network into subgraphs. Each subgraph was called a component and characterized by overlapping relations between the single-objective local optima networks and the multi-objective Pareto optima network. Results on multi-objective landscape problems showed that most Pareto optimal solutions were reachable from the single-objective local optimal solutions. This tendency was emphasized by increasing the number of objectives and the objective correlation. The number of co-variables impacted the number of cross-link relations between the single-objective local optima networks and the multi-objective Pareto optima network. The results suggested that searching for single-objective problems is a clue to multi-objective optimization.

</details>


### [107] [MuEvo: LLM-Driven Evolution of Multi-Heuristic Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03636)
*Haoze Lv,Ning Lu,Shengcai Liu,Shaofeng Zhang,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: MuEvo是一个由LLM驱动的启发式集成进化框架，通过动态组件管理和LLM驱动的协同进化来共同优化多个交互组件，在四个组合优化领域中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-AHD方法主要优化单一启发式，而实际优化框架通常依赖多个交互组件。直接扩展单启发式方法面临两个挑战：早期组件选择可能忽略后期才显现潜力的组件，而独立进化则忽视了组件间的依赖关系。

Method: MuEvo包含两个核心模块：(1) 动态组件管理——使用短预算探测和可逆生命周期来在搜索过程中修正组件优先级；(2) LLM驱动的协同进化——通过多种群集成评估、跨组件信息共享、关系引导的配对进化和自适应预算分配来协调组件种群。

Result: MuEvo在四个组合优化领域的选择超启发式和组件化蚁群优化中进行了评估。结果表明MuEvo持续改进人工设计的框架，并优于最先进LLM-AHD方法的多组件扩展版本。

Conclusion: MuEvo在控制器介导的启发式池和功能分化算法组件方面均表现出有效性，证明了其在多组件启发式集成进化中的潜力。

Abstract: Large language model-based automated heuristic design (LLM-AHD) has shown strong potential in discovering effective heuristics for combinatorial optimization problems. However, existing methods primarily optimize a single heuristic, whereas practical optimization frameworks often rely on multiple interacting components. Directly extending single-heuristic methods is challenging because early component selection can overlook components with late potential, while independent evolution ignores inter-component dependencies. We propose MuEvo, an LLM-driven framework for evolving heuristic ensembles under ensemble-level feedback. MuEvo combines Dynamic Component Management, which uses short-budget probing and a reversible lifecycle to revise component priorities throughout the search, with LLM-Driven Co-Evolution, which coordinates component populations through Multi-Ensemble Evaluation, Cross-Component Information Sharing, Relation-Guided Pair Evolution, and Adaptive Budget Allocation. We evaluate MuEvo on selection hyper-heuristics and componentized ant colony optimization across four combinatorial optimization domains. Results show that MuEvo consistently improves human-designed frameworks and outperforms representative multi-component extensions of state-of-the-art LLM-AHD methods, demonstrating its effectiveness across both controller-mediated heuristic pools and functionally differentiated algorithmic components.

</details>
